kaṭaloṭu uḻanta paṉittuṟip paratava   									          (patiṟṟuppattu 8: 3-4)
eṉṟu patiṟṟup pattu ciṟappittuk kūṟuvatu ārāyattakkatu.  ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, neṭuñcēralātaṉ, kuṭṭuvaṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷ maṭṭumalla cērarkaḷē kaṭaṟpōril vallavarkaḷāka iruntaṉar.  avarkaḷ vallavarkaḷ maṭṭumalla villavarkaḷākavum iruntu vantaṉar.  ataṉi
iru munnīrt turuttiyuḷ
muraṇi yōrt talic ceṉṟu
kaṭampu mutal taṭinta kaṭuñciṉa muṉpiṉ cēraṉ - 
					(patiṟṟuppattu. II 10: 2-4)
eṉṟu kūṟuvatum cintikkat takkatu.
ki.mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē cōḻarkaḷ valimi vāynta kaṭaṟpaṭiyi uruvākki viṭṭaṉar.  kāvirip pūm
paṭṭiṉattiliruntu āṭci naṭattiya karikāṟ cōḻaṉ tam aṭalēṟu pōṉṟa kaṭaṟpaṭiyiṉiṉṟu īḻanāṭṭi veṉṟu vāki cūṭiṉāṉ.  perum kaṭaṟ paṭiyik koṇṭa cēṉiyik, karikālaṉ veṉṟatip pukaḻntu veṇṇik kuyattiyār pāṭuvatu cōḻarkaḷiṉ kaṭaṟpaṭic ciṟappi aṟiyat takkac cāṉṟākum.
naḷiyiru munnīr nāvā yōṭṭi
vaḷitoḻi lāṇṭa vuravōṉ maruka
kaḷiyiyal yāṉik karikāl vaḷava
ceṉṟamark kaṭanta niṉ ṉāṟṟa ṟōṉṟa
veṉṟōy niṉṉiṉu nallavaṉaṉṟē	(puṟam 66)
ki.pi.11 -ām nūṟṟāṇṭil mutalām irācarācaṉ īḻattiyum mullit tīvukaḷ palavaṟṟiyum cēra nāṭṭiyum taṉ kaṭaṟpaṭi koṇṭu veṉṟatiyum irācēntira cōḻaṉ antamāṉ, nakkavāram kāḻakam, cāvakam mutaliya palvēṟu kiḻakkāciya nāṭukaḷiyum taṉ kaṭaṟpaṭi koṇṭu māperum kaṭaṟpaṭi veṟṟiyiyīṭṭi ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟati yārē maṟukka vallār?  mutalām irācēntiraṉ mutalām kulōttuṅkaṉ ākiyavarkaḷ kiḻakkāciyāvil peṟṟa kaṭaṟpaṭi veṟṟi varalāṟṟup pukaḻ peṟṟatākum1.
caṭiyavarmaṉ parāntaka pāṇṭiyaṉ ciṟanta kaṭaṟpaṭiyi niṟuviyiruntāṉ.  ki.pi.835-il cīmāṟaṉ cīvallavaṉiṭam ciṟanta kaṭaṟpaṭi iruntatu.  mutal vāramaṉ kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉ (ki.pi268-1311)  taṉ kaṭaṟpaṭiyiṉ valimiyāl mūṉṟām parākkirama pākuvi veṉṟu mīṉak koṭiyi tirukkōṇamaliyiṟ poṟittuḷḷāṉ.
taḷapatikaḷ
tamiḻarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ paṭikaḷi tāṉippaṭi, yāṉippaṭi, tērppaṭi, parippaṭi, kaṭaṟpaṭi eṉṟu pirittu ov voru paṭikkum taḷapatikaḷ niyamittiruntaṉar.  ovvoru paṭiyilum uḷḷa ovvoru piriviṟkum talivaṉ niyamikkap paṭṭiruntāṉ.  aracaṉē ellāp paṭikaḷukkum mēlāṉa taḷapati yāka (Supreme commander) āka iruntu vantāṉ.  pōri aracaṉē muṉṉiṉṟu naṭattuvāṉ.  cilacamayam taḷapatikaḷ pōri muṉṉiṉṟu naṭattuvar. aracaṉ akkālattil pācaṟiyil pōr veṟṟi peṟuvataṟkuriya vaḻikaḷi āyntu āvaṉa ceytu koṇṭiruppāṉ.  avaciyam eḻuntāl aracaṉ pōrmuṉikkuvantu tam paṭikaḷi vaḻi naṭattuvāṉ.
taḷapatikaḷ perumpālum iḷavaracaṉ allatu aracaṉuṭiya uṟaviṉarkaḷākavē iruppar. aracaṉ iṟantuviṭṭāl avaṉ makaṉ ciṟuvaṉāka iruntālum muṭi puṉintukoṇṭu araca kaṭṭili aṭivāṉ.  avaciyam eḻuntāl avaṉ ciṟuvaṉ eṉṟeṇṇātu pōrmuṉikkup pōntu paṭikaḷi naṭat tuvatum uṇṭu.  iḷam maṉṉarkaḷip pōrukkup pōka vēṇṭām eṉṟu aṉṉi, amiccar, aracakurukkaḷ ākiya varkaḷ taṭuppatumuṇṭu.  āṉāl avarkaḷukkut takka camātāṉam kūṟi pōrmuṉikku aracaṉmakaṉ celvāṉ.  avaṉ pōril uyir tuṟappatum uṇṭu.  veṟṟi peṟuvatum uṇṭu.
aracikaḷum, amiccar maṉivikaḷum, piṟa kuṭippeṇṭir kaḷum taṅkaḷukku orēyoru makaṉ iruntālum añcātu makaṉip pōrukkuc cellat tūṇṭi vāḻtti vāḷum villum ampum aḷittu viṭi koṭuttu aṉuppuvar. pōril tanti kaṇavaṉ uṭaṉ piṟantārkaḷ ākiya aṉivarum uyir tuṟantu taṉakkum taṉ kuṭumpattiṟkum kulak koḻuntāka irukkum 16-vayataṭinta orē makaṉiyum tamiḻ aṉṉi tayaṅkātu pōrukku aṉuppiṉāḷ.  makaṉ iruntāl vīramakaṉ eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ irukkavēṇṭum. atu perumi. kōḻi makaṉ eṉṟu iruppatu taṉakkuc ciṟumi, kuṭumpattiṟku iḻivu eṉṟu eṇṇiṉāḷ.  pōril taṉ makaṉ puṟamutuku kāṭṭi ōṭi vīḻntāṉ eṉṟāl vīṭṭil uḷḷa avaṉatu aṉṉi atik kēṭkac cakiyātu cāyvāḷ; vaṭakkiruntu cāvāḷ.
paṭit talivarkaḷ
tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa aintu vakip paṭikkum taṉittaṉiyē paṭittalivarkaḷum ellāp paṭikkum periya taḷapatiyum iruntu vantaṉar.  cēra nāṭṭil maṉṉarkaḷiṉ uṟaviṉarkaḷ paṭittalivarkaḷāy iruntaṉar.  avarkaḷ nāyaṉ eṉa aḻikkap paṭṭaṉar.    iṉṟu avarkaḷ nāyar eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.  tamiḻ nāṭṭil paḷḷarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kulattalivarkaḷi nāyaṉ eṉṟum nayiṉār eṉṟum nintē eṉṟum iṉṟum aḻippar.  peṇkaḷi nācciyār eṉṟum navilvārkaḷ.  āṉāl piṟa iṉattavarkaḷ evvaḷavu celvam paṭittavarkaḷāka iruntālum avarkaḷi nāyaṉ eṉṟu paṇṭik kālap paḷḷarkaḷ kūṟār.
paṭṭaṅkaḷ
paṭivīrarkaḷukkum paṭittalivarkaḷukkum aracarkaḷ pala paṭṭaṅkaḷ aḷittu vantaṉar.  avaṟṟil ēṉāti eṉpatum oṉṟu.  appaṭṭattiṟkuriya mōtiram ēṉāti mōtiram eṉappaṭum.  kalit tokiyil ippaṭṭam kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.1
aracaṉ taḷapatiyāy irupuravikaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟa tēr mītu ivarntu celvāṉ.  pōrkkaḷam pukumuṉ kuḷittu āṭi aṇikaḷ pūṇṭu malar cūṭit taṉ tāyiṭam aṉumati peṟṟu avaḷ vāḻttutalip peṟṟu pōrk kōlam pūṇṭu vāḷum villum ēnti tam kulateyvatti vaḻipaṭṭu nālvakip paṭikaḷum cūḻap pōrkkaḷam pukuvāṉ.  tērmītu veḷḷik kuṭikaḷum paṉṉiṟak koṭikaḷum paṟakkum.  ellāvaṟṟiṟkum mēlē nāṭṭuk koṭi paṟakkum. kitērnta tērppākaṉ tēric celuttuvāṉ.  avaṉ kavacam aṇintu kiyil villēnti vīrāppuṭaṉ celvāṉ.  pōrmuracu, ekkāḷam, veṟṟic caṅku pōṉṟavikaḷi icikkum icivāṇar kūṭṭam piṉ toṭarum.  cila camayam taḷapati yāṉimītu amarntu pakivarkaḷ mītu pāyvatum uṇṭu.  aṟupatu vayatuḷḷa mutirnta uṟuti vāynta kompukaḷiyuṭiya ciṟanta yāṉi mītē aracaṉ ēṟic celvāṉ.  yāṉi mītuḷḷa irukki ampāri eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭum.  aracaṉ āṇiyiṭṭāl yāṉi añcātu etiyum ceyyum.  muṟṟukiyiṭappaṭṭa, kōṭṭik katavukaḷi yāṉi muṭṭi mōti uṭittu paṭi vīrarkaḷ uṭpuka vaḻi ceyyum.  yāṉippākaṉ kiyil kuttuk kōlum tōṭṭiyum vittiruppāṉ.  pōr maṟavarkaḷ koṭikaḷi vīcik koṇṭu arukil aṇiyaṇiyāy niṟpar.  aracaṉ taṉ mārpiṉ mītu pakivarkaḷiṉ koṭiya kūriya ampukaḷ tuḷikka muṭiyātavāṟu pulit tōlāl ceyyap peṟṟa kavacam aṇintiruppāṉ.  yāṉi mītu amarntu cellum aracaṉ mārpil pakivarkaḷiṉ ampō vēlō pāyntu viṭṭāl aracaṉ añcātu avikaḷit taṉ mārpiṉiṉṟu piṭuṅki, taṉ uṭampiṉiṉṟu utiram poṅkip perukuvati poruṭpaṭuttātu pakivarkaḷ mītu mārpiliruntu piṭuṅkiya ampip pāyccuvāṉ. pakivar ampu aracaṉiṉ mutukiṉ pakkam pāyntu viṭṭāl aracaṉ pōrukkup payantu puṟamutukukāṭṭi ōṭum poḻutu paṭṭatāka eṇṇappaṭum.  eṉavē tamiḻaka maṉṉarkaḷ mutukup pakkamalla vilāppuṟam pakivarkaḷiṉ pāṇaṅkaḷ pāyntu viṭṭālum piṉ oru noṭiyum uyirtari yārkaḷ.  mārpil pakivarkaḷ pāṇam pāyvatu maṉṉarkaḷukkum piṟa vīrarkaḷukkum māṇpit taruvatāka eṇṇappaṭṭatu.

3. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ pōr muṟi
tamiḻarkaḷ toṉmiyāṉa iṉattavarkaḷ.  avarkaḷ paṇpu mikkavarkaḷ avarkaḷ paṇṭu toṭṭu pōril īṭupaṭṭiruntārkaḷ eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu.  avarkaḷ tākkuvataṟkum taṟkāp
piṟkum paṭikaḷum araṇkaḷum paṭikkaruvikaḷum amit
tiruntaṉar.  taṟpātukāppuk keṉṟu nakaric cuṟṟi aṇi aṇiyāy araṇkaḷ amittiruntaṉar.  araṇkaḷ mītu poṟikaḷ vittirun
taṉar.  araṇkaḷic cūḻntu akaḻikaḷ veṭṭi atil mutalikaḷi viṭṭiruntaṉar.  paṭikaḷum paṭikkaruvikaḷum uruvākki irun
taṉar.  āriyarkaḷ intiyāvil aṭiyeṭuttu vippataṟku muṉpē iṅku kōṭṭikaḷum kottaḷaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa.  irukku vētattil pakivarkaḷiṉ (tācarkaḷiṉ) cempuk kōṭṭikaḷ  irumpuk kōṭṭikaḷip paṟṟi kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  tamiḻarkaḷ kiṟittava ūḻikku muṉpē pala araṇkaḷi amit tiruntaṉar eṉṟu tamiḻil uḷḷa paḻam perum nūṟkaḷāṉa tol kāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷiṉ mūlam teḷikiṉṟōm eṉṟu potuvākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
āṉāl tamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟikaḷi āynta purucupuṭ, pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār, irāmaccantira tīṭcitar pōṉṟavarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ kallil palvēṟu vakiyāṉa āyutaṅkaḷic ceytu payaṉpaṭutti vantuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.  avvāyutaṅkaḷ avarkaḷ vilaṅkup pōrkaḷil kiyāṇṭu veṟṟi yīṭṭiyavikaḷ eṉṟu terikiṉṟaṉa.  avi kōṭari, vācci, uḷi, katti, curaṇṭi, īṭṭimuṉi, vil, taṭi, ulakki pōṉṟavikaḷ eṉṟu terikiṉṟaṉa.
avarkaḷ vaṭa intiyāvil putiya kaṟkālattik kaṭantu ulōka kālatti aṭinta poḻutu veṇkalattilāṉa mīṉ eṟivēl, kuttuvāḷ, katti, vācci, uḷi, tamarūci, mūvilivēl (tiricūlam), taṭi, ulakki pōṉṟa palvēṟu āyutaṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttivantaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  arappaṉ paṇpāṭṭil avar
kaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya veṇkalattilāṉa īṭṭi, kōṭari, kaṇṭa kōṭari, katti, uḷi, curaṇṭi pōṉṟavi kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
teṉ intiyāvil ātitta nallūr paṇpāṭṭil kiṟittava ūḻikku muṉ makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya irumpiṉālāṉa vēl, kampu (spear - īṭṭi) mūvili vēl, kaṇṭa kōṭari, palvēṟu vakiyāṉa īṭṭi muṉikaḷ (Spear heads) vāḷ (Sword) irupuṟamum vāyuḷḷa vāḷ (Double edged sword) ciṟiya kuttuvāḷ (Small dagger) mīṉeṟi vēl (Lance) ampu muṉi pōṉṟa kūriya nuṉiyuṭiya eṟivēl (Barbed Javelin) oru ariya āyutam (A curious weapon) ciṟiya īṭṭikaḷ (Small Rances) kuttuvāḷ (Dagger - cilthŸ) uḷi, kik kōṭari (Hatchet), palvēṟu vakiyāṉa kattikaḷ, kōṭari, taṭikaḷ vaḷinta kattikaḷ (Curved Knives) pōṉṟa palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa āyutaṅkaḷ muṉṉūṟṟiṟku mēṟpaṭṭavikaḷ akaḻntu kaṇṭu avikaḷit tol poruḷ kāṭci cāliyil iṭam peṟac ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  avikaḷip paṟṟi nūl eḻuti āyutaṅkaḷiṉ pukip paṭaṅkaḷi iṇittu viḷakkam tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1
tamiḻil irāmāyaṇam, pāratam pōl itikācaṅkaḷō, pōrkkāviyaṅkaḷō tamiḻarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭavilli.  iṉṟuḷḷa paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil paṇṭit tamiḻar payaṉpaṭuttiya āyutaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ peritum kāṇappaṭavilli.  ciṟpaṅkaḷilum nikaṇṭukaḷilum akarātikaḷilum kāṇappaṭum pōrkkaruvikaḷukkuk kūṭa ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam viḷakkam peṟa muṭiyavilli.
tamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟu ciṟappāka inta irupatām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil cērar, cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar, pallavar eṉṟa peyarkaḷāl cilarāl cila varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ variyap peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa.  avikaḷil tamiḻaka maṉṉarkaḷ āṟṟiya pōrkaḷip paṟṟi viḷakkantarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  muṟkāla, piṟkālat tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷ kumari toṭṭu imayamvari pōriṭṭu veṟṟi peṟṟuḷḷārkaḷ.  āḻkaṭal kaṭantu ciṅkaḷam, puṭpakam, cāvakam, kāḻakam pōṉṟa pala nāṭukaḷukkut tam kappaṟpaṭi mūlam paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu veṉṟu vil, mīṉ, puli, ciṅkam, erutu pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷ poṟitta koṭikaḷi viṇmuṭṭa paṭṭoḷi vīcip paṟakkumpaṭi ceytaṉar.  imayamaliyil tamiḻar koṭi ēṟṟappaṭṭatu.  kuṭi nili nāṭṭappaṭṭatu.  tirukkōṇamaliyil tamiḻar koṭi paṟakka viṭappaṭṭatu eṉṟellām palappala ceytikaḷ collappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.  āṉāl enta varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷum tamiḻarkaḷ eytiya inta veṟṟikaḷ ententap pōrkkaruvikaḷāl peṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu viḷakkantaravilli.  ivarkaḷukku takavalkaḷē kiṭikka villiyā?  allatu maṟantu viṭṭārkaḷā?  allatu mantirattāl im māperum veṟṟikaḷi īṭṭiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu karutiṉārkaḷā eṉpatu teriyavilli.  avarkaḷ eppaṭik karutiṉālum karutaṭṭum.  avarkaḷ eppaṭik karutuvataṟkum avarkaḷukku urimiyuṇṭu.  āṉāl avarkaḷ karuttit tam varalāṟṟu nūṟkaḷil kuṟip
piṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatē em paṇivāṉa karuttākum.
varalāṟṟiṉ mukkiyamāṉa amcam pōrkaḷ.  pōrukku iṉṟimiyāta karuvi  pōrkkaruvikaḷ.  pōrō ciṟiya pōr alla.  kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷōṭu naṭattiya caṇṭi, caccaravu pōṉṟa
talla.  akkālattil naṭanta pāratap pōriviṭa, irāmāyaṇap pōri viṭa mika mikap periya pōr.  eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka ki.pi 10-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil irāca rāca cōḻaṉ kālattil irācēntira cōḻaṉ talimiyil cōḻarkaḷiṉ tarippaṭiyum, kutirip
paṭiyum, yāṉippaṭiyum, īṭṭip paṭiyum, kappaṟpaṭiyum intiyāvi lum ilaṅkiyilum maṭṭumalla antamāṉ, nikkōpār, ilakkattīvukaḷ, kāḻakam (kaṭāram), maliyam, ciṅkapuram, tāylāntu, kampōcakam, ciṅkapuram, cāvakam, cirivicayam, māyiruṭiṅkam, māppāḷam, vaḷipanturu,  talittakkōlam, ilāmūri, ilāmūri tēcam pōṉṟa pala nāṭukaḷi veṉṟu pulik koṭiyi nāṭṭi
ṉāṉ.  inta māperum pōril veṟṟiyīṭṭiya cōḻar perum
paṭi eṉṉeṉṉa pōrkkaruvikaḷip payaṉ paṭuttiṉa eṉṟu 
ituvari enta varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyatāka namakkut teriyavilli. cōḻar varalāṟṟi virivāka eḻutiya 
tiru. kē. nīlakaṇṭa cāttiri kūṭa tam nūlil pōrk karuvikaḷip paṟṟi viḷakkam kūṟavilli.  pāṇṭiyar varalāṟṟil kūṭa paṭik
kalaṉkaḷ vicayattil avar pārāmukamāy iruntu viṭṭār.
iḵtaṉṟi cōḻar varalāṟṟip paṟṟi eḻutiya ṭākṭar irāca māṇikkam, tiru. catācivappaṇṭārattār pōṉṟavar pōrk karuvikaḷik kāṇat tam kaṇkaḷi iṟuka mūṭik koṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟē terikiṟatu.  cērar varalāṟṟi varintavarkaḷum, pallavar kaḷ varalāṟṟi varintavarkaḷ nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyār vaḻiyil maṉṉarkaḷiṉ camayam, avarkaḷ kaṭṭiya kōyilkaḷ paḻutupārtta kōyilkaḷ,  avarkaḷiṉ maṉiviyar, makkaḷ, ēṟpaṭuttiya nipantaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyē mīṇṭum mīṇṭum eṭuttuk kāṭṭi yuḷḷārkaḷē yoḻiya cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya, pallava aracarkaḷ āṟṟiya pōr muṟikaḷ avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiyap pōrkkaruvikaḷ mutaliyavi
kaḷi eḻuta avarkaḷiṉ eḻutukōl iṭam aḷikkā tiruntati eṇṇi ikkāla māṇavarkaḷ- ēṉ?  varuṅkāla māṇavar kaḷum varuntātirukka muṭiyātu.
araṇkaḷ
araṇkaḷ makkaḷiṉ taṟpātukāppiṟkāka amikkappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa.  araṇkaḷ aracaṉ vāḻum kōnakaraṅkaḷilum piṟa mukkiya māṉa nakaraṅkaḷilum amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  araṇ eṉpatu nakaric cuṟṟi uyaramāka eḻuppappaṭṭa matilēyākum.  itaṉi matilaraṇ eṉṟu kūṟuvar.  ivvaraṇ ceṅkallāl kaṭṭappaṭṭu mēlē cutiyāl pūcappaṭṭirukkum. piṟkālattil kaṟkaḷālum amikkappaṭṭiruntatu.  kallāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa araṇkaḷukkuc cāntāka cempi urukki vārttu uṟutiyāṉatākac ceyyappaṭ ṭiruntatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
pakivarkaḷ ēṟutaṟkariya uyarnta matilkaḷāka araṇkaḷ irukkum.  pakivarkaḷ tolivil varum poḻutē kāṇpataṟ kuriya uyarnta kōpuraṅkaḷ matil mēl amikkap peṟṟiruntaṉa.  pakivaṉ talinakari muṟṟukiyiṭum poḻutu noccip pōr iyaṟṟa muṭiyātāyiṉ pakivarkaḷ aṟiyātu talinakariliruntu veḷiyē karantu celvataṟkuriya vaḻikaḷ curuṅkikaḷ (curaṅkap pātikaḷ) iruntaṉa.
araṇkaḷukku vāyilkaḷ irukkum.  cila araṇkaḷukku iraṇṭu vāyilkaḷ uṇṭu.  cila araṇkaḷukku nāṉku vāyilkaḷum uṇṭu.  itaṉik kōṭṭi vāyilkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvar.  inta vāyilkaḷ uṟutiyāṉa marak katavukaḷālāṉatu.  katavukaḷiṉ mītu eḵkut takaṭukaḷ vittu naṉṟākat aṟiyappaṭṭirukkum.  iravil inta vāyilkaḷ pūṭṭappaṭuvatum uṇṭu.  kōṭṭi vāyilkaḷi vīrarkaḷ imi koṭṭātu niṉṟu pātukāttu varuvārkaḷ.
araṇic cuṟṟi akaḻi veṭṭappaṭṭirukkum.  anta akaḻi yil nīr pāycci atil mutali, cuṟāmīṉ pōṉṟavikaḷ viṭap paṭṭirukkum.  kōṭṭi vāyilkaḷil maṭṭum marap palaki
yiṉālāṉa pālaṅkaḷ pōṭappaṭṭirukkum.  antap pālaṅkaḷiṉ
vaḻiyākavē makkaḷ nakarukkuḷ pōy varuvar.  pōrkkālaṅkaḷil ippālaṅkaḷi uḷḷē iḻuttuk koḷḷuvārkaḷ.  pakivarkaḷ akaḻiyil iṟaṅkiyē araṇi aṇuka vēṇṭiyatāyirukkum.  pakivarkaḷ akaḻiyik kaṭantu nūlēṇi vaḻiyāka matil mītu 
ēṟuvar.
araṇ vakikaḷ
nakarip pātukākka araṇkaḷ palavakippaṭṭatāka iruntaṉa.  talinakarp pātukāppiṟku araṇkaḷ 5 vakiyākap pirikkap
paṭṭiruntatu (1) matilaraṇ (2) nilavaraṇ (3) kāṭṭaraṇ (4) nīr araṇ 
(5) maliyaraṇ eṉappaṭum.
matil araṇ vakikaḷ
ellā araṇkaḷilum matilaraṇkaḷē nalla pātukāp puṭiyaṉa.  inta matilaraṇ 4 vakippaṭṭatāy iruntatu.  (1) matil (2) eyil (3) iñci (4) cō eṉappaṭum.  matilaraṇi potuvākak kōṭṭi eṉṟum purici eṉṟum aḻippar.  purici eṉpatu purintiruppatu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum.  purital vaḷital allatu cūḻtal eṉappaṭum.  araṇkaḷ aracaṉ vāḻum akanakaric cūḻntiruppatāl purici eṉappaṭṭatu.
nālvakiyāṉa araṇkaḷuḷ uyarattāl periyatu matilaraṇ eṉappaṭum.  uyaramum akalamum vāynta araṇ eyil eṉappaṭum.  uyaram, akalam, tiṇmi vāyntatu iñci eṉappaṭum.  immūṉṟu taṉmiyōṭu mikavum arumiyāṉa taṉmikaḷ palavum cērntatu cō eṉappaṭum.  araṇkaḷil cō araṇē taliciṟantatākum.  araṇiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟavanta vaḷḷuvar uyaram, akalam, uṟuti, pakivarkaḷāl aḻikka muṭiyāta aruḷ ākiya nāṉku taṉmikaḷ vāyntatāka araṇkaḷ irukkum eṉṟu nūlōr kūṟuvar eṉṟār.1 cempiyurukki cāntākak koṇṭu karuṅkal vittuk kaṭṭap
paṭṭa matilaraṇiyuṭiyatu iñci eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu.2 inta iñciyaraṇ ilaṅkiyil iruntatu.  tuvārakā camuttiram (tuvari nakaril) iñciyaraṇ iruntatu.  cempu araṇ irāvaṇaṉukkuc contamāṉatu.  itu ilaṅkiyil iruntatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  iḵtaṉṟi cilappatikārattil cuḻalaḻaluḷ vikiṉṟu (Match locko)nrh” eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatāl maturiyil cōvaraṇ eṉṟa araṇ iruntatāka eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu.
makkaḷiṉ pātukāppiṟkāka araṇkaḷ eḻuppap paṭṭiruntatu.  araṇkaḷiṉ pātukāppiṟkāka akaḻkaḷum araṇkaḷ mītu pala ariya poṟikaḷum (iyantiraṅkaḷum) amikkappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa.  araṇkaḷ mītu pala vil vīrarkaḷ iravum pakalum imikoṭṭātu etirikaḷi vīḻtti emaṉulakam aṉuppat tayārāy niṉṟaṉar.  araṇ mītu eḻuppap peṟṟa kāval kōpuraṅkaḷiṉ mītu kāval vīrarkaḷ pakivarkaḷ tolivil varum poḻutē aṟintu taṅkaḷ paṭit
talivaṉukku muṉṉaṟivippuc ceyya eppoḻutum kaṇkāṇittu vantaṉar.
matilkaḷiṉ mītu ariya poṟikaḷ vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  maturi mānakaril puṟa matilil irunta poṟikaḷum karuvi
kaḷum uṟuppukaḷum paṟṟi cilappatikārattil viḷakkamākak kūṟap
paṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
intap poṟikaḷil pala tamiḻ nāṭṭilē ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.   cila yavaṉarkaḷiṭamiruntu vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṭa eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  
tamiḻakattiṉ talinakaraṅkaḷic cuṟṟi oṉṟiṉi aṭuttu oṉṟāka aṭukkaṭukkāka pala kōṭṭikaḷ kaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭap peṟṟiruntaṉa.  veḷippakutiyil eḻuppap peṟṟa araṇkaḷ puṟa matilkaḷ eṉṟum uṭpuṟattil uyarttap peṟṟa araṇkaḷ aka matilkaḷ eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa.  aka matilkaḷi viṭa puṟa matilkaḷ ceymuṟiyil ciṟappuṟṟilaṅkum.  puṟamatilkaḷiṟṟāṉ palavakip poṟikaḷ amikkappaṭṭirukkum.  akaḻum, puṟa matilum akamatilum evvāṟu iruntaṉa eṉpataṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka iṉṟiya tañci peruvuṭiyār kōyil amippum akamum namakku nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ilaṅkukiṟatu.  araṇi yoṭṭi uṭpuṟattil uḷḷa mēṭikku akappā eṉṟu peyar.
matiluḷḷa nakarkaḷ, perumpālum kōṭṭi, puri, purici, eyil, kaṭakam eṉṉum pataṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟit taṉpeyarōṭu iṇittu viḷaṅkum.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi , ceṅkōṭṭi vicayāpuri, aḻakāpuri, maruṅkāpuri pōṉṟatāka irukkum.
araṇkaḷiṉ vāyilkaḷ akaṉṟum uyarntum irukkum.  vāyil katavukaḷ uṟutiyāṉa kaṉatta palakiyāl amikkappaṭṭu mēlē kaṉatta irumput takaṭukaḷ iṇikkap peṟṟirukkum.  vāyilkaḷ, tiṟami mikka kāval vīrarkaḷāl eppoḻutum kākkap peṟṟu vantatu.   pakivarkaḷiṉ oṟṟarkaḷum vīrarkaḷum urukkarantu uṭ pukuntu uḷḷaruppu vēli ceyya viṭātu kāval vīrarkaḷ kāttu niṟpar.  araṇik kaṭantu nakarukkuḷ pukum ovvoru variyum ovvoru poruḷkaḷiyum kāvalarkaḷ aiyamaṟac cōtittu aṟinta piṟakē uṭpuka aṉumati aḷippar.  vāyilkaḷil mēli nāṭṭu yavaṉa vīrarkaḷ kāval vīrarkaḷāka niṉṟu tamiḻ nāṭṭu araṇkaḷik kāttu vantuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu cilappatikāram, mullip pāṭṭu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.1
iṭiccaṅka kālattil irunta pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ kō nakarāṉa kapāṭapuram uyariya araṇkaḷiyuṭiyatāka iruntatu.  itaṉ vāyiliṉ katavukaḷ poṉṉālāṉatu.  ataṉ katavukaḷ muttālum maṇiyālum piṟa ariya poruḷkaḷālum aṇi ceyyappeṟṟu viḷaṅkiyatāka vāṉmīkiyiṉ vaṭa moḻi irāmāyaṇam kūṟukiṟatu.1
araṇkaḷiṉ vāyilkaḷil, pakivarkaḷ araṇi aṇuka muṭiyātu eṉpati cuṭṭikkāṭṭa, kāṟcilampu, taḻiyāṭi, pantu, pāvi, iṉṉōraṉṉa peṇkaḷukkuriya āṭi, aṇi mutaliya poruḷkaḷit toṅka viṭṭiruppar.  amar āṟṟi araṇi aṭiya iyalātu.  ati maṟantu tirumpic celluṅkaḷ.  āci koṇṭu vantāl āvi tuṟappīrkaḷ allatu aṭipaṭṭu tōṟṟu ōṭuvīrkaḷ.  uṅkaḷ vīrakkaḻalik kaḷintu kāṟcilampi  aṇiyac ceyvōm.  mārpuk kavacattip paṟittu taḻiyuṭi pūṇac cey vōm.  uṅkaḷ kiyil uḷḷa vāḷiyum vēliyum akaṟṟi āṭi makiḻuṅkaḷ eṉap pantum pāviyum tantu makaḷirkaḷākki māṉatti iḻakkac ceyvōm eṉa aṟivippatu pōl irukkum.  itu araṇil uḷḷē vāḻpavarkaḷiṉ  āṇmiyik kāṭṭum.2
vāyil katavukaḷ turuvēṟātu neypūcap peṟṟirukkum.  katavukaḷil māṉ, aṉṉam pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap peṟṟu makkaḷiṉ kalic ciṟappi viḷakkik kāṭṭā niṟkum.  cēra aracaṉiṉ, toṇṭi nakarak kōṭṭi vāyilil uḷḷa katavukaḷil mūvaṉ eṉṟa pakivaṉuṭiya paṟkaḷum mattiya eṉpāṉuṭiya paṟkaḷum veṇmaṇi vāyil eṉṉum cōḻa nāṭṭu nakara vāyiṟ katavil avaṉuṭiya pakivaṉ eḻiṉiyiṉ paṟkaḷum poṟittu vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
pakivarkaḷ tolivil varuvati aṟiyumāṟu araṇkaḷ mītu nāṟṟiciyilum mika uyarnta aṭṭāli eṉṉum kōpuraṅkaḷ pala eḻuppap peṟṟiruntaṉa.  avaṟṟil kāval vīrarkaḷ iruntu koṇṭu iravu pakal imi koṭṭātu kāttu varuvārkaḷ.  ivarkaḷ aṭṭālic cēvakar eṉappaṭuvar.  avarkaḷ irukkum uyarnta iṭam pārval irukki eṉappaṭum.3
pācaṟikaḷ
talinakariṉiṉṟu atikat tolivil pōr naṭantāl pācaṟi amittup pōr naṭattappaṭum.  pācaṟiyic cuṟṟi muḷvēli kōlap peṟṟirukkum.  avi vēṉiṟkālattil amik
kappaṭṭāl vēṉiṟ pācaṟi eṉṟum kuḷirkālattil amikkap peṟṟāl kūtaṟ pācaṟi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.  maṟṟak kālaṅkaḷil nīṇṭa nāḷ pōr etuvum naṭantatākat teriyavilli.  pōr, kār kālam ārampam ākumuṉ allatu ārampam āṉavuṭaṉ muṭikkap
paṭum.
aracaṉ vīraruṭaṉ pācaṟiyil taṅki iruppāṉ.  paki aracaṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟa aracaṉ taṉakkum tampaṭi vīrarkkum pātukāvalāka pōr mukattiṟkup piṉ pācaṟikaḷ amippāṉ.  aṅku kāṭukaḷi veṭṭi paṭikaḷ taṅkum iṭam amippāṉ.  ataṉip pāṭivīṭu eṉṟu kūṟuvar. terukkaḷ oḻuṅ
kāka amintirukkiṉṟa pācaṟiyiṉ vīṭukaḷ taḻiyāl vēyap
paṭṭirukkum.  yāṉip paṭiyum, viṟpaṭiyum pācaṟiyik kāval ceytu niṟkum.  kōṭṭikaḷi viṭap pātukāval uḷḷa kūṭāraṅkaḷil naṭuvil aracaṉ amarntirukkum kōyil uṇṭu.
iṅku makaḷirkaḷ, iṭiyil vāḷ aṇintu iravum pakalum viḷakkukaḷ avintu viṭātapaṭi neyyum tiriyum ēntit tirivar.  iṅku yavaṉa vīrarkaḷ kāval kāppatum uṇṭu.  pācaṟiyic cuṟṟip palamāṉa kāval iṭap peṟṟirukkum.  poḻuti nāḻiki vaṭṭiliṟkaṇṭu uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ aṟivikkum aluvalarkaḷum iruppar.  muntiya nāḷ pōriṉi niṉittu tuyil koḷātu irukkum pōr vīrarkaḷum taḷapatikaḷum iruppar, yāṉikaḷum kutirikaḷum pōriṉi niṉintu nittiriyiṉṟi niṟkum.
aracaṉ naḷḷiravil meykkāvalarōṭu puṇpaṭṭa vīrar
kaḷip pārttu āṟutal kūṟuvāṉ.  maruntukaḷ kaṭṭuvāṉ.  paṭi vīrarkaḷ kāyamuṟṟāl maruntukaḷ aḷikka maruttuvarkaḷum avarkaḷukku utavi ceyyat tātikaḷum iruppar.  ivikaḷi yellām viḷakkamāka neṭunalvāṭi eṉum caṅka nūlilum piṟa caṅka nūlkaḷilum kāṇalām.
nāḷ pārttal
pōriṭuvataṟku maṉṉaṉ nāḷum kōḷum nimittamum pārppāṉ.  maṟavarkaḷ kuṟipārppar - aracaṉ vāṉa nūṟ pulavarkaḷi aḻittu nalla nāḷ kuṟittu anta nāḷil paṭikaḷōṭu pōrukkup pōvāṉ.  annāḷ avaṉukku vacati illāviṭil annāḷil kōṭṭi vāyilukku veḷiyē taṉ kuṭiyiyum vāḷiyum aṉuppi vippāṉ.  itu kuṭi nāḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭum.  vāḷ nāḷ kōḷ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.  cila camayam araṇiṟku appāl muraciyum aṉuppum vaḻakkam uṇṭu.  potuvākap pōrukkup paraṇi naṭcattiram ēṟṟatāka eṇṇappaṭum.
maṟavarkaḷ, pōrukkup puṟappaṭa nālvaki nimittam pārppar.  atāvatu, (1) vāyppuḷ (2) uṉṉam (3) puḷ (4) viricci eṉappaṭum.  (1) vāyppuḷ eṉappaṭuvatu taṟceyalākap piṟar vāyiṉiṉṟu kēṭkap
paṭum maṅkala moḻi.  itaṉi iṉṟu makkaḷ acarīr vākku eṉkiṟārkaḷ. (2) uṉṉam eṉpatu oru kuṟippiṭṭa uṉṉamaram taḻittuvariṉ aracaṉukku veṟṟi eṉṟum atu paṭṭup pōyiṉ aracaṉukkut tōlvi eṉṟum eṇṇik koḷvar (3) puḷ eṉappaṭuvatu ānti, kūki mutaliya paṟavikaḷ olikkum taramum, kākam, vallūṟu mutaliya puḷḷiṉam paṟakkum tikkum paṟṟik kuṟittuk koḷḷum kuṟi (4) viricci eṉappaṭuvatu pōrvīrarkaḷ pōrukkup puṟappaṭṭup pōy ōriṭattil taṅki irukkum poḻutu naḷḷiravil ōrttuk kēṭkum vāṉac col.  iḵtaṉṟi aracarkaḷiṉ maṉivimārkaḷ makappēṟu peṟum kālaṅkaḷil pōr ceytal tamiḻar kaṇṭa marapaṉṟu.
pōrukkup pōvataṟku iraṇṭoru nāḷkaḷukku muṉpu aracaṉ pōrukkup paṭi tiraṭṭuvāṉ.  vaḷḷuvaṉ yāṉi mītēṟi muracaṟintu talinakariluḷḷa makkaḷi pōrukku varumāṟu aṟivippāṉ.  pōr pukumuṉ maṉṉaṉ maṟavarkaḷukku ūkkam ūṭṭum poruṭṭu oru viruntaḷippāṉ.  atu peruñ cōṟṟu nili eṉappaṭum.  appāl aracaṉ pōr vīrarkaḷukku aṭiyāḷap pūkkaḷum pōrp pūvum paṭikkalaṅkaḷum vaḻaṅkuvāṉ.  ciṟanta paṭittalivarkaḷukkup paṭṭaṅkaḷum, vīramikka pōr maṟavar
kaḷukkup poṉ malarum aḷippāṉ.  pōril taṉakku veṟṟi niccayam eṉṟu kaṇṭāl pakivar nāṭṭik kippaṟṟum muṉpē atit taṉ iravalarkkum paṭittalivarkaḷukkum paṅkiṭṭuk koṭuppatum uṇṭu.
pōrukkup puṟappaṭum nāḷ vantu viṭṭāl tali nakariṉ akamum mukamum putup polivuṭaṉ tōṉṟum.  eṅkum kōlā kalamāka irukkum.  veṟṟic caṅku olikkum; pōr muracukaḷ muḻaṅkum; pōr muracaṉṟi palvēṟu muracaṅkaḷum ceviṭupaṭa ōci eḻuppum; ekkāḷam, kompu pōṉṟa kuḻal olikaḷ eṅkum kēṭkum.
pōr vīrarkaḷ nīril mūḻki, vaṭṭuṭi uṭutti, paṭikkalan tāṅki, taliyil neyyiṭṭu nīvi malar cūṭi, aṇivakuttu koṟṟavikkup paliyiṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭu vañciṉam kūṟi neṭu moḻi pakarntu āravārittu nakarukku valamākap puṟappaṭṭuc celvar.  tuṟakka vāḻvi nāṭum vīra maṟavarkaḷ poṟṟakaṭṭi vāyil iṭṭuk koṇṭu pōr muṉikkup pōvatum uṇṭu.  pōr
vīrarkaḷ pakivarkaḷiṉ nāṭu, nakar, ūr mutaliyavikaḷil uḷḷa nalla poruḷkaḷic cūṟiyāṭuvar.  māṭa māḷiki, maṇṭapam mutaliyavikaḷi iṭittut takarppar. (itu piṟkālat til eḻunta vaṉmuṟi.  itu tamiḻar paṇpaṉṟu).
akkālattup pōrk karuvikaḷip paṟṟip paṭikaḷi eypaṭi, eṟipaṭi, kuttuppaṭi, veṭṭuppaṭi, aṭipaṭi, taṭupaṭi eṉa āṟuvakiyākap pirittuḷḷaṉar.  (1) villiṉāl ampi eypavarkaḷ koṇṭapaṭi eypaṭi eṉappaṭum.  (2)vēl koṇṭu pakivarkaḷ mītu eṟiyum paṭi viṭṭēṟu eripaṭi eṉappaṭum.  (3) īṭṭi, kuntam mutaliya karuvikaḷ koṇṭu pakivar
kaḷit tākkum paṭi kuttup paṭi eṉappaṭum.  (4) kikaḷil vāḷ ēnti pakivarkaḷit tuṇṭittum veṭṭiyum pōrpuriyum paṭi veṭṭuppaṭi (5) maḻu, taṇṭu mutaliya karuvikaḷ koṇṭu pakivarkaḷ mītupāyntu tākkum paṭikku aṭipaṭi eṉappaṭum.  (6) kavacam kēṭakam mutaliyavikaḷāl pakivarkaḷiṉ tākkutal
kaḷittāṅkik koṇṭu añcātu pakivarkaḷ mītu cāṭi avarkaḷi muṟiyaṭikkum paṭi taṭupaṭi eṉappaṭum.  atōṭu tamiḻar paṭiyil erimaruntukaḷ iruntataṟkuc cāṉṟu kaḷum uṇṭu.  ippoḻutu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum vāṇam tamiḻ nāṭṭil uḻiñip pōril upayōkittiyirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇap
paṭukiṟatu.
pōr arumpi viṭṭāl muṉṉaṇiyil tāṉippaṭi (kālāṭpaṭi) aṇivakuttuc cellum, avikaḷi aṭuttu kutirippaṭiyum ataṟkup piṉ tērppaṭiyum iṟutiyil yāṉippaṭiyum aṇivakuttu aṇi aṇiyāyc cāri cāri
yāyc cellum.  ovvoru paṭiyilum kaṇiyaṉ iruppāṉ.  ivaṉi cēṇik kaṇimakaṉ eṉpar.  potuvākak kaṇiyaṉi cēṉip peruṅkaṇi eṉpar.  pōr taṉatu nāṭṭil naṭantālum piṟa nāṭṭil naṭantālum aracaṉ puṟappaṭum poḻutu avaṉōṭu aṟivarkaḷ, pulavarkaḷ, pāṇarkaḷ, kūttarkaḷ pōṉṟōr uṭaṉ celvatu vaḻakkam.  cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭa nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttuc cellum poḻutu avaṉuṭaṉ nūṟṟiraṇṭu nāṭaka makaḷirum irunūṟṟeṭṭu kuyiluvarkaḷum (vāttiyakkārarkaḷum) nūṟu naki vēḻamparkaḷum ceṉṟaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
pōrkkaḷam
pōrkkaḷattiṟku kaḷam, kaḷaṟi, mutu nilam, paṟantali eṉṟel lām kūṟappaṭum.  pōr ārampam ākum muṉṉar pōrkkaḷattiṟku aṇmiyil uḷḷa ūriluḷḷa antaṇarkaḷ, āniṟikaḷ, peṇkaḷ, ciṟuvarkaḷ, mutiyōr, piṇiyāḷar mutaliya
varkaḷ aṅkiruntu akaṉṟu pātukāppāṉa iṭaṅkaḷukkup pōy viṭumāṟu eccarikki ceyyappaṭum.  pōr toṭaṅkiṉāl aracarkaḷ yāṉip paṭi koṇṭu pakivarkaḷiṉ aṇikaḷip piḷantu pakivarkaḷik kīḻē taḷḷi mitittut tuvippar.  aracaṉ taṉ paṭikaḷi aṇivakuttu caturam, nīḷcaturam, vil, vaḷiyam, vaṭṭam, arivaṭṭam, cakkaram, ciṟiviri paṟavi mutaliya palvēṟu vaṭivil niṟuvi pōril pakivarkaḷip puṟamutuku kāṭṭi ōṭumpaṭic ceyya muyalvar.  pōr naṭakkum poḻutu pōr muracu olikkum.  pakivar
kaḷiṉ paṭit talivaṉ, pakivarkaḷiṉ aracaṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷiṉ talikaḷik koytu koṇṭu varum vīraṉukku maṉṉaṉ matippiṭaṟ
kariya paricukaḷ vaḻaṅkuvāṉ.  itu tali mārāyam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.  pala nāḷ pōrāyiṉ pakalil pōr naṭakkum.  pakalavaṉ maṟintatum paṭikaḷ pōri niṟuttiviṭṭut tattam pācaṟi cellum.
pōr niyati
tāṉippaṭi pakivarkaḷiṉ tāṉippaṭiyoṭu pōriṭum.  avvāṟē kari, pari, tēr eṉum paṭikaḷum etirikaḷiṉ kari, pari, tērp paṭikaḷōṭē pōrāṟṟum paṭi maṟavarkaḷ muṉṉar celvar.  avarkaḷi naṭattum paṭittalivar piṉ celvar.  aracaṉ iṟutiyil celvāṉ.  ituvē niyatiyum pōr marapum ākum.  cila camayam itu māṟi iruppatum uṇṭu.  pōril tōlviyuṟum nili eḻuntāl aracaṉ paṭik kaḷattiṉiṉṟum pācaṟiyiṉiṉṟum pakivarkaḷukkut tūtu aṉuppuvatum ati māṟṟaracaṉ ēṟṟu uṭaṉ paṭikkikaḷ ceytu koḷvatum uṇṭu.  ōrōr camayam tūtaṉic ciṟippaṭuttuvatum, peṇvēṭam pūṇac ceytu tiruppiyaṉuppu
vatum uṇṭu.  ivi varampu kaṭanta ceyalākum.  atāvatu ilaṭciyap pōr oḻukkattiṟku muraṇpaṭṭaviyākum.
pōril veṟṟi peṟṟa aracaṉ tōlviyuṟṟa aracaṉiyum avaṉatu makaṉiyum vilaṅkumāṭṭit taṉ muṉ koṇṭuvarac ceytu avarkaḷ iruvar tali mayirkaḷiyum ciṟikkac ceytu iruvar talikaḷilum vaṭṭa vaṭivamākac cāṇiyi vittu naṭuvil eṇṇey vārttu tiriyiṭṭu viḷakkēṟṟi naṭiviḷakkāka araṇmaṉiyiṉ vāyiliṉ irupuṟamum, niṟkac ceyvatuṇṭu.  ivvāṟu cōḻaṉ oruvaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉiyum avaṉ makaṉiyum ceytatākak kūṟappaṭuvatuṇṭu.  pōr maṉitaṉi vilaṅkuttaṉmi peṟac ceykiṟatu eṉpataṟku ivi cāṉṟukaḷ.
tamiḻarkaḷiṉ nīti nūl koḷkikaḷukku māṟākavum pōr oḻukkattiṟku māṟākavum cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ naṭantu koṇṭatiṉ palaṉāka tamiḻ nāṭu palaṅkuṉṟi aṭimiyāyiṟṟu.  mokalāyar āṭcikkum nāyakkar āṭcikkum marāṭṭiyar āṭcikkum āṅkilēyar pirañcukkārar āṭcikkum āṭpaṭṭu tāḻcciyuṟṟatu.
veṟṟi peṟṟa vēntaṉ tōlviyuṟṟa aracaṉ araṇ maṉiyil vīrāpicēkam ceytu koḷvatuṇṭu. tōlviyuṟṟa nāṭṭil veṟṟit tūṇ nāṭṭuvāṉ.  mali iruntāl ataṉ mītu kuṭiyi nāṭṭuvāṉ.  koṭiyip poṟippāṉ.  taṉ āṇip paṭi naṭakkum vari anta nāṭṭil taṅki iruppatumuṇṭu.
pōr muṭintatum pōr vīrarkaḷukkup paricaḷippāṉ.  kāyamuṟṟavarkaḷukku utavi nalkuvāṉ.  iṟantu pōṉavarkaḷiṉ kuṭumpattiṟku utavuvāṉ.  vīrarkaḷukku viruntaḷippāṉ.  pulavar kaḷukkum pāṇarkaḷukkum, viraliyarkaḷukkum paricaḷippāṉ.  pōril uyir tuṟanta tiyākikaḷukkum paṭittalivarkaḷukkum avarkaḷiṉ ūr, peyar, pōr, cīr iṉṉōraṉṉa kāriyaṅkaḷip poṟittu kal naṭuvatuṇṭu.  itu naṭukal eṉappaṭum.  naṭṭa kalliṟku nīrvārttu, neyvārttu, pūccūṭi kōyil eṭuttu viḻāk koṇṭāṭappaṭuvatuṇṭu.
makkaḷukkāka, nāṭṭiṟkākap pōril uyir tuṟanta vīraṉukku naṭukal nāṭṭi avaṉit teyvamāka eṇṇi makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar.  pōril uyir nītta vīraṉukku naṭukal nāṭṭi kōyil eṭuppittu avvīraṉi vaḻipaṭṭu varuvatu tamiḻ marapu.  vaḷḷuvar, tam potu maṟiyil,
eṉimuṉ nillaṉmiṉ tevvir palareṉi
muṉniṉṟu kal niṉṟavar 			(kuṟaḷ. 771)
eṉṟār.  itaṉ poruḷ pakivarē!  eṉṉuṭiya talivaṉ muṉ etirttu niṟkātīr.  eṉṉuṭiya talivaṉ muṉ etirttu niṉṟu maṭintu kal vaṭivāy niṉṟavar palar eṉpatākum.  itaṉ mūlam iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tam vīrari niṉippūṭṭumāṟu kal nāṭṭip peyar poṟittu vittatu ulakam pōṟṟum ceyalākum.
iṉṟu namatu nāṭṭu aracāṅkap putip poruḷ ārāyccit tuṟiyiṉ ittakiya naṭukaṟkaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṇṭeṭuttuḷḷa ṉar.  avikaḷ namatu tol poruḷ kāṭci cāliyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  naṭukal nāṭṭum vaḻakkam tolkāppiyar kālat tilē iruntu varum oru tamiḻ marapākum.
kāṭci kal kōḷ nīrppaṭi naṭutal
cīrttaru ciṟappiṟ perumpaṭi vāḻttaleṉ
ṟiru mūṉṟu vakiyiṟ kalloṭu  
(tol. poruḷ 60 :  19-21)
pōril vīḻnta vīrarik kallil amittu vaḻipaṭum vaḻakkam toṉṟu toṭṭu iruntu varukiṟatu.  niṉivuk kaṟkaḷ arukil vīrarkaḷiṉ āyutaṅkaḷi vittu teyvamākap pōṟṟap paṭṭu vantati caṅka nūṟkaḷ naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.1
pōrkkoṭumi
pōr koṭiyatē;  eṉṟālum piṟa paṇṭi nāṭṭu makkaḷip pōlavē tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ ataṟkuc cila kaṭṭup pāṭu
kaḷi vitittaṉar.  akkaṭṭuppāṭṭōṭu naṭattum pōr aṟappōr eṉappaṭṭatu.  paṇṭu toṭṭu paki aracaṉip piṭittu varuvatum avaṉatu maṉivi makkaḷic ciṟipiṭippatum paki aracarkaḷatu araṇmaṉikaḷi aḻittu atik kaḻuti pūṭṭiya ēṟiṉik koṇṭu uḻutu eḷḷum koḷḷum erukkum karukkum vitippatum pakivarkaḷ nāṭṭil uḷḷa makkaḷiṉ illaṅkaḷi yellām iṭittut takarttu avarkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷi yellām cūṟiyāṭuvatum avarkaḷic cittiravati ceyvatum avarkaḷiṉ payir paccikaḷi yellām tīyiṭṭup pocukkuvatum aṟappōr ākātu.  āyiṉum avi naṭi peṟṟu vantaṉa eṉpataṟkiyamilli.
tōlviyuṟṟa maṉṉaṉi muṭitarikkac ceytu taṉ muṉ koṇṭu vantu maṇṭiyiṭṭu niṟkac ceytu avaṉatu maṇi muṭiyik kāl koṇṭu iṭaṟuvatum,  avaṉatu maṉivi makkaḷiṉ mārpukaḷi aṟuppatum, pakivarkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷip piṭuṅkuvatum pallip piṭuṅki kōṭṭi vāyiliṟ patittu vippatum tali
kaḷi veṭṭi makkaḷ aṉivarum kāṇumāṟu toṅka viṭuvatum tōlviyuṟṟa aracaṉiyum avaṉatu putalvaṉākiya paṭṭat
tiḷavaracaṉiyum moṭṭiyaṭittut taliyiṉ mītu vaṭṭamākac cāṇiyi vittu neyvārttu tiriyiṭṭu nerup pēṟṟi naṭi viḷakkāka veṟṟi peṟṟa aracaṉ araṇmaṉiyiṉ vāyilil irupuṟamum niṟkac ceyvatum koṭuñ ceyalkaḷākum.  ivi aṟappōr ākā.  nākarika makkaḷ ceyyum pōrākavum karutappaṭa māṭṭātu.  tamiḻ nāṭṭil vāḻnta mūṉṟu aracarkaḷil cōḻaṉ veṟṟi peṟum poḻutu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭit tīyiṭṭup pocukki, araṇmaṉi vīṭukaḷ aṉittiyum iṭittut takarppāṉ.  cila āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu tōlviyuṟṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ palamuṟṟup patil cōḻa nāṭṭi aḻippāṉ.  iraṇṭu āṇṭu ceṉṟu cōḻaṉ palam peṟṟu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiyum cēra nāṭṭiyum aḻippatu pōṉṟa koṭuñceyalāl tamiḻakam palamiḻantatu pāḻpaṭṭatu.  mokalāyarkaḷum, nāyakkarkaḷum marāṭṭiyarkaḷum, āṅkilēyar
kaḷum tamiḻakatti āḷa nērntatu.  tamiḻ nāṭṭu mūvēntarkaḷum pōriṭātu oṉṟu paṭṭu iruntāl āṅkila ēkātipattiyatti
viṭa oru perum tamiḻar ēkātipatti yatti varalāṟu kaṇṭirukkum.
pōrppayiṟci
tamiḻarkaḷ irāṇuvak kallūrikaḷum āyutak kiṭaṅkaḷum paṇṭu toṭṭu amittu vantaṉar.  paṭip payiṟci aḷikka āciriyarkaḷ iruntaṉar.  vīrarkaḷukkum taḷapatikaḷukkum iḷavaracar
kaḷukkum aracarkaḷukkum nalla payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa.  ovvoruvarum maṟpōrum paṭikkalap pōrum payiṉṟu vantaṉar.  maṟpōr payilvataṟku āṅkāṅkē pala pēravikaḷum muraṇ
kaḷarikaḷ eṉum payiṟcik kūṭaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa.  (kēraḷam - kaḷari) 
murukkamarattiṉ parutta aṭimaratti nilattil naṭṭu ataṉ mēl paṭikkalaṉkaḷi eṟintu porunar eṟipaṭip pōr payiṉṟaṉar.  araṇmaṉiyi aṭutta ceṇṭu veḷi, viyāḷi vīti eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭum.  anta veḷi nilattil vāci vāriyar eṉpōr kutirikaḷukku iru cārikaḷum aiṅkatikaḷum patiṉeṇ maṇṭilaṅkaḷum payiṟṟi vantaṉar.  avarkaḷuḷ talivar araca vāriyār eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭuvār.  
kāḻōr vātuvar eṉpōr yāṉikaḷip pōrttuṟiyil payiṟṟuvōr āvar.  tamiḻarkaḷ yāṉip pōrkku naṉku payila vēṇṭum.  atuvum pala āṇṭu nalla āciriyarkaḷiṭam payila vēṇṭum.  yāṉip pōri naṭatta karicāttiramum kutirip pōril īṭupaṭa acuva cāttiramum payiṉṟaṉar.
atē pōla kappaṟ pōrukkum nalla payiṟci peṟa vēṇṭiya tuṇṭu.  kappal pōrukku nīriyal, vāṉiyal, vaḷi iyal, iyantira nuṭpam mutaliyavikaḷiyellām payila vēṇṭiyatiruntatu.  pōrkkaḷi kappal kaṭṭuvataṟku oru taṉip payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭu vantatu.  kaṭaṟpōril peritum neytal nilap paratavarkaḷ īṭupaṭṭu vantaṉar.
tamiḻakattil āyutaṅkaḷ ceyvataṟkuk kollarkaḷ iruntaṉar.  āyutaṅkaḷ nalla pātukāppākavum oḻuṅkākavum vikka āyutac cālikaḷ iruntaṉa.  aracaṉatu paṭikkalaṅkaḷi vippataṟku taṉi iṭam iruntatu.  atu paṭikkalak koṭṭil eṉappaṭum.  aṅku pōrkkaruvikaḷukku neypūci pīliyaṇintu māli cūṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
pōrkkaruvikaḷ
ātikālattil tamiḻakattil pōrkkaruvikaḷ etuvum tōṉṟa
villi.  ātiyil tamiḻakattil eḻunta kaṟkālamakkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta tēvikaḷukkākak kallip payaṉ paṭutti vantaṉar.  antak kaṟkāla makkaḷ nākarikam iṉṉa teṉpati aṟiyārkaḷ.  avarkaḷiṉ tēviyō mikak kuṟivu.  avarkaḷukku iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷ uṇavu.  eṉavē uṇavip peruvataṟ kuriya kaṟkaruvikaḷē avarkaḷukkut tēviyāy iruntatu.  eṉavē kal, katti, kalvāḷ, kal uḷi, kal kōṭāri, cikki mukkikkal (neruppiyuṇṭākkum kal) ākiyavikaḷē avarkaḷukku avaciya māṉa poruḷāka iruntaṉa.  ivikaḷik koṇṭē avarkaḷ maraṅkaḷi veṭṭi taṭi, kampu, kaṭṭumaram, ōṭam, maravāṭi, kalviḷakku mutaliyavikaḷic ceytu koṇṭaṉar.  avarkaḷ kampāl villic ceytu ataṉ nāṇil kalli vittu paṟavi
kaḷiyum, ciṟiya vilaṅkukaḷiyum kāykaṉikaḷiyum vīḻttiṉar.  icik karuvikaḷi uruvākkiṉar.
appāl ulōka kālattilē makkaḷ uyarntaṉar - mākkaḷi ṉiṉṟu uyarntu makkaḷ niliyi aṭintaṉar.  ulōka kālam iṭattiṟku iṭam vēṟupaṭṭatāy eḻuntatu.  tamiḻakattil āti kālattilē irumpuk kālam arumpiyatāka eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  cintu veḷiyil ārampa kālattilē veṇkalak kālam eḻuntirukka lām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  inta ulōka kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ katti, arivāḷ, arivāḷ maṇi, kōṭari, vēl, ulōkattalip puḷḷi ampukaḷ, vilkaḷ, tōṭṭi, kēṭayaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa karuvi kaḷi uruvākkiṉar.  inta ulōka kālattilē makkaḷ naṉi ciṟanta nākarikaṅkaḷik kaṇṭaṉar.  vāḷil pala vakikaḷiyum vēliṟ palavakikaḷiyum, kattiyil pala vakikaḷiyum taṭikaḷil palavaki kaḷiyum kēṭayaṅkaḷil palavakikaḷiyum uruvākkiṉar.  ik kālattilē taṉiyuṭimi eḻuntatu.  uḻutal toḻil vaḷarntatu.  kittoḻil malintatu.  itaṉ payaṉāy pōrkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.  makkaḷ oruvari yoruvar veṉṟu koṉṟu uṭimikaḷip perukka niṉintaṉar.  eṉavē avarkaḷ vakivakiyāṉa pōrk karuvikaḷic ceytu kuvikka muṟpaṭṭaṉar.
koṭikaḷ
nāṭṭukku nāṭu vevvēṟu vaṇṇattil vevvēṟu ciṉṉaṅkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭatāka, toṉmiyāṉa kālantoṭṭu tamiḻ nāṭṭil koṭikaḷ iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.  pōr vīrarkaḷukku pōril uḷḷuyirppum, uṇarccik kaṉivum ūṭṭuvatu koṭiyē yākum.  koṭi, oṟṟumi, nāṭṭuppaṟṟu, māṉam, mariyāti ākiya vikaḷiṉ ciṉṉamāka matikkappaṭum.  koṭiyiṉ paṇput tiṟaṅ kaḷiṉ ceyalāṭci, pōr vīrarkaḷ mītu oru mūlātāramāṉa āṟṟal kūṟāka amintirukkiṟatu.  pakivarkaḷāl koṭi kip paṟṟik koḷḷappaṭuvatu nāṭṭiṉ allatu nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ cāvu maṇiyākak karutappaṭum.  potuvākak koṭi, tuvajam, patāki, kētaṉā, vijayanti eṉṟellām aḻikkappaṭum.  cumār 2500-āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tamiḻakattil maṉṉarkaḷ koṭiyip payaṉ paṭutti vantuḷḷaṉar.  tolkāppiyam eṉṉum paḻam perum nūlil aracarkkuriya iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷkaḷ paṭi, koṭi, kuṭi, muracu, kutiri, yāṉi, tēr, tār, muṭi ākiyavi eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
paṭiyum koṭiyum kuṭiyum muracum
naṭinavil puraviyum kaḷirun tērum
tārum muṭiyum nērvaṉa piṟavum
terivu koḷ ceṅkōl aracarkkuriya 
					(tol. poruḷ 1563)
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatu cintikkattakkatu.  tamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟu pāṇṭiyarkaḷukku mīṉ koṭiyum cōḻarkkup pulik koṭiyum cērarkku vil koṭiyum pallavarkkuc ciṅkam, erutu mutaliya koṭikaḷum cāḷukkiyarkaḷukkup paṉṟik koṭiyum iruntaṉa eṉpati meyppaṭuttukiṟatu.  iḵtaṉṟi irāvaṇaṉ icik karuviyākiya yāḻit taṉ koṭiyil poṟittiruttāṉ eṉṟum aṟikiṉṟōm.  paṇṭiyat tamiḻakattil palavitak koṭikaḷ iruntataṟkuc cilappatikārattilum, kaliṅkattupparaṇiyilum pala caṅka nūṟkaḷilum cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu.  koṭi makkaḷukku uṇarcciyi
yūṭṭi putiya ūkkam eḻap, putiya nalla uyirk kāṟṟi nukarac ceytatu.  tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷ īḻatti veṉṟu tirukkōṇa mali mītu taṅkaḷ koṭikaḷip poṟitaṟkum, vaṭa intiyāvi veṉṟu imayamali mītu taṅkaḷ kuṭiyi nāṭṭiyataṟkum cāvakam puṭpakam ākiya nāṭukaḷi veṉṟu aṅkellām taṅkaḷ veṟṟik koṭiyip paṟakka ceytataṟkum pala cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu.
tamiḻ nāṭṭu aracarkaḷ taṅkaḷ koṭiyi taṅkaḷ uyirāka matittaṉar.  kumari mutal imayam vari veṟṟi peṟṟa tamiḻ nāṭṭu mūvēntarkaḷum imayattiṉ mītu taṅkaḷ koṭiyi poṟittatum kuṭiyi nāṭṭiyatu uṇṭu.  pāṇṭiyaṉ oruvaṉ īḻatti veṉṟu tirukkōṇamaliyil taṉatu iraṭṭi mīṉ poṟitta araca koṭiyip poṟittāṉ.  atu iṉṟum aḻiyātu kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu.  ilaṅki varalāṟṟil aḻiyāc ciṉṉamāka iṭam peṟṟu ilaṅkukiṟatu.  kaliṅkattup paraṇiyil cōḻarkaḷatu veṟṟiyik kuṟippiṭum poḻutu palavitak koṭikaḷ kuṟippiṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.  paṉṟi, mēḻi, kali, ciṅkam, yāḻ, vil, naṇṭu mutaliyavikaḷ kīḻē iṟakkappaṭṭu cōḻarkaḷiṉ pulik koṭi uyarttap paṭṭatu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.1
pōr muracu
araca ciṉṉaṅkaḷākiya kulam, peyar, muṭi, kōl, māli, kaṭṭil, kuṭi, koṭi, muttiram, kaṭimaram, kutiri, yāṉi, tēr, maṉi, muracu ākiya patiṉintil muracu pōrkkaruvikaḷil mukkiya māṉatu.  araca kaṭṭaḷiyoṉṟi aṟivittaṟku aṭikkappaṭum perumpaṟi muracu eṉappaṭum.  muracil mūṉṟu vaki uṇṭu.  (1) koṭi muracu (2)  muṟi (niyāya) muracu (3) pōr muracu eṉappaṭum.  ivaṟṟuḷ pōr muracu ciṟantatu.  itaṟkut taṉiyāka oru kaṭṭil uṇṭu.  atu muracu kaṭṭil eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
pōr muracu pōrukku makkaḷit tiraṭṭa nakaril payaṉ paṭuttappaṭum.  pōril porunari ūkkuvippataṟkup pōrk kaḷattil aṭikkappaṭum.  veṟṟi peṟṟa pōtum itu iṭiyōci yeṉa olikkappaṭum. inta vīra muraciṟkup puliyi  veṉṟa kāḷiyiṉ cīvāttōlē pōrttap peṟum.
puṉimarup paḻuttak kuttip puliyoṭu porutu veṉṟa
kaṉikura lurumuc cīṟṟak kataḻviṭi yurivi pōrtta
tuṉi kural muracam
eṉṟu cintāmaṇi kūṟuvatu kāṇka.  pōr muracu teyvat taṉmi yuḷḷatākavum veṟṟiyit taruvatākavum karutappaṭṭatāl ataṟku māliyum pīliyum aṇiyappaṭum.  paliyiṭappaṭum ati nīrāṭṭi vaḻipaṭuvatum uṇṭu.
araca kōṭṭaṅkaḷil pōr muracu araca kaṭṭilil vikkap paṭṭirukkum.  pōr muraciṉi eppoḻutum vīrarkaḷ kāval kāttu niṟpar.  pōr muraci piṟar toṭṭāl avar uyir uṭaṉē paṟikkappaṭum.  pōr muracu vikka eṇṇeyiṉatu nuriyi mukantāṟ pōṉṟa melliya pūviyuṭiya kaṭṭil iruntatu.  pōr ārampikku muṉ mayil pīliyum nīla niṟattiyuṭiya māliyum poṟṟaḷiyiyuṭiya māliyum cūṭṭap peṟṟu kurutippali koḷḷum viruppattiyuṭiya vīramuracu nīrāṭi varum.  maṉṉarum māvīrarum muraci vaḻipaṭṭu pōrkkaḷam koṇṭu celvar.  pōr muracip pakivarkaḷ kippaṟṟa vīrarkaḷ eḷitil iṭam aḷiyār.  pōr muracip pakivarkaḷ kippaṟṟi viṭṭāl paṭikaḷ aḻintatu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum.  oru vīraṉ uyiruḷḷavari pōr muracu kāppāṟṟappaṭum.
pōr oḻukkam
paṇṭiyat tamiḻarkaḷ pōrilum cila paṇpuvāyntavarka ḷāyt tikaḻntaṉar.  pōril vīrarkaḷ vilaṅkukaḷ pōl naṭavātu oḻukkamuṭiyōrkaḷāy viḷaṅkumpaṭi avarkaḷukkuc cila pōr oḻukkaṅkaḷ kaṟpikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  naṭimuṟiyil avvāṟē iruntaṉa eṉṟu koḷḷutal varalāṟṟup piḻiyākum.  pōrk kaḷattil tamiḻ vīrarkaḷ tam mutukil pakivarkaḷiṉ tākkutalāl puṇpaṭac cakiyār.  atu oru vīraṉ pōril puṟamutukiṭ ṭōṭi
yatik kuṟikkum eṉṟum atu vīraṉuṭiya ilakkaṇat tiṟkum oḻukkattiṟkum iḻivu taruvatākavum eṇṇappaṭṭatu.  akamāt
tāka vilāppuṟattil pakivarkaḷiṉ pāṇaṅkaḷ vantu tākkiṉum atu mutukil tākkiyatākavē karutappaṭum.  tamiḻ vīraṉ mutukil aṭipaṭṭālō allatu mutukil aṭipaṭṭiruppatākap piṟar karutiṉālō ataṟkup piṉ oru noṭip poḻutum uyar vāḻa oruppaṭāṉ.  tamiḻ maṟavarkaḷ tam mukattiṉum mārpiṉum koḷḷum puṇkaḷi viḻuppuṇ eṉṟu ciṟappittu kūṟuvar.  avvāṟu viḻuppuṇ paṭāta nāḷip piṟavāta nāḷākak koṇṭu  tamiḻ maṟavarkaḷ pētuṟuvar.  paṇṭiya nākarika makkaḷ aṉi varumē ivvāṟu karutiṉar eṉpataṟkuk kirēkka rōma vīra kāviyaṅkaḷ cāṉṟākum.  vēntaṉ tam vīrarkaḷi pōrukkut tūṇṭātu kālam eḻa villi eṉṟu vāḷā iruppiṉ vīrarkaḷ pōrt tiṉavu koṇṭu tammiṟ poṟutāyiṉum iṟakkalām eṉṟu ārpparittu tam tōḷkaḷit taṭṭik kokkarittu niṟpar.  tōyaṉmāṟaṉ eṉṉum ciṟṟaracaṉ īrntūri āṇṭu vantāṉ.  avaṉ pala pōrkaḷil īṭupaṭṭu avaṉ uṭampellām pala taḻumpukaḷ niṟintiruntaṉa.  avvaṭukkaḷ avaṉ uṭampiṟku aḻaku ceyvaṉavākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. avvaḻaku kuṟṟamaṟṟa āṇmi niṟinta vīraṉukkuriya aḻaku.   muṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ viḻuppuṇ paṭā uṭampi aḻakaṟṟa uṭampākak karutiṉar.  ati kuṟṟamuṭiya uṭalākak kūṭak karutiṉar.1 ikkaruttukkaḷ pōril vīravuṇarvu tōṉṟa mikip paṭuttip parappappaṭṭaṉa.
pōrkkaḷattil muṉ vitta kālip piṉ viyāta māvīraṉ pakivarkaḷiṉ ampukaḷukkum vēlkaḷukkum añcātu avi
kaḷi mārpil ēṟṟu viḻuppuṇpaṭātu maṟakkuṭi vīraṉ maraṇam aṭintāl avar uyir tuṟakkam aṭivatilli eṉṟa nampikkiyip paḻaṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ koṇṭiruntaṉar.  itu pōlavē kirēkkarkaḷ pōril uyir nīttavarkaḷ maṭṭum elisiyam eṉṟa ulakil peruvāḻvu vāḻvārkaḷ eṉṟu nampiṉar.  viḻuppuṇ paṭātu vēntaṉ iṟantu paṭṭāl avaṉ mēṉi taḻumpupaṭāta kuṟṟam uṭiyatu eṉṟa iḻukki aṭiyātu irukka avaṉ uṭalit taruppip pulliṟkiṭatti antaṇar kūṭi niṉṟu pōril uyir tuṟantār cēravēṇṭiya vīra tuṟakkattil ivaṉum pukuka eṉṟu vāḷāl avaṉ mēṉiyil vaṭuc ceytu appāl avvuṭali aṭakkam ceyvārkaḷ.
oṭal marīiya pīṭil maṉṉar
nōyppāl malinta yākki taḻīik
kātal maṟantavar tītu maruṅ kaṟumār
aṟampuri koḷki nāṉmaṟi mutalvar
tiṟampuri pacumpuṟ parappiṉ kiṭappi
maṟaṅkan tāka nallamar vīḻnta
nīḷkaḻal maṟavar celvuḻic celkeṉa
vāḻ pōḻn taṭaṅkalu muyntaṉar mātē - puṟam 93
intap pāṭṭiṉiṉṟu pōrkkaḷattil porutu maṭiyātu araṇmaṉiyil iruntu piṇivāyppaṭṭu iṟappatu kūṭa maṉṉarkku iḻivu eṉṟu muṟkālattil tamiḻarkaḷ karutiṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  pōrukkut tūṇṭuvataṟkāka inta nampikkikaḷ ūkkuvikkap
paṭṭaṉa.
pōr ārampikku muṉ pakivar  nāṭṭiluḷḷa antaṇarkaḷ, aṟavōrkaḷ, peṇkaḷ, kuḻantikaḷ, nōyāḷikaḷ, īmak kaṭaṉ kaḻikka makkaḷip peṟātōr mutaliyavarkaḷi annāṭṭi viṭṭu otuṅkik koḷḷum paṭi muṉṉeccarikki ceytaṉar.  pōrukku añci veḷivarātu patuṅkum maṉṉaruṭaṉ pōr iṭuvatu vīrattiṟku iḻukkeṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ karutiṉar.  ivi aṟa nūṟkaḷil kūṟappeṟṟa ilaṭciyap pōr muṟiyākum.

4. ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ
ilakkiyam
tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkum tamiḻ moḻikkum iṉṟu kiṭittirukkum pērum perumiyum cīrum ciṟappum, ataṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷip poruttatē yākum.  nāṭṭu varalāṟṟiṟkum, kalivaralāṟṟiṟkum moḻi varalāṟṟiṟkum eḻuttu varalāṟṟiṟkum aṭippaṭiyāy iruppatu namatu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyam illi.
namatu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ perumpālum, ilakkaṇaṅkaḷākavum aṟa nūṟkaḷākavum kāppiyaṅkaḷākavum pakti nūṟkaḷākavum amintuḷḷaṉa.  perumpālāṉa paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ aka vāḻkkiyic ciṟappittuk kūṟum nūṟkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pōr, pōrmuṟikaḷ, pōrkkaruvikaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ peritum kāṇappaṭavilli eṉṟu ayal nāṭṭa ṟiñarkaḷ cilar kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.  atu tavaṟu eṉṟō poy eṉṟō mikipaṭak kūṟiya kūṟṟeṉṟō nām eḷitil puṟakkaṇittu viṭuvataṟ
killi.  akanāṉūṟṟilum puṟanāṉūṟṟilum āṅkāṅkuk kāṇap
paṭum pōrkaḷ, vīrac ceyalkaḷ, aracarkaḷiṉ tīrac ceyalkaḷ, pōr muṟikaḷ, aṟappōrkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷic cila aṟiñarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟārkaḷ.  āṉāl avikaḷi enta aṟiñarkaḷum pōr ilakkiyaṅkaḷāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷa māṭṭārkaḷ.  vaṭa moḻiyil uḷḷa makāpāratam irāmāyaṇam pōṉṟa iti kācaṅ
kaḷō, pōr ilakkiyaṅkaḷō, pōr varalāṟukaḷō pōrk kāppiyaṅkaḷō tamiḻil illi eṉṟu pala aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.  ipperuṅkuṟiyi nīkkuvataṟkeṉṟē kampaṉum villiyum vaṭa moḻiyiṉiṉṟu irāma kātiyiyum villi pāratattiyum tamiḻ ākkam ceytaṉar eṉṟu cila tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.   tamiḻil uḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil pōr ilakkiyamāka cila aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟum orēyoru nūl kaliṅkattup paraṇiyākum.1 atu caṅkakāla ilakkiyam alla.  mikap piṟkāla nūl.  atu ki.pi.1070 - il muṭipuṉinta mutaṟkulōttuṅkaṉ kaliṅka nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttu veṟṟi
īṭṭiyatip pukaḻntu avaṉatu avip pulavarākiya cayaṅ
koṇṭāṉ pāṭiya oru nūlākum.  itaṉip piṟkālattil tōṉṟiya pōr ilakkiyamākak karutalām.  inta nūlil kūṭa tamiḻarkaḷiṉ pōr muṟikaḷiyum pōrkkaruvika ḷiyum paṟṟi viḷakkamāṉa ceytikaḷik kāṇa muṭiyavilli.
nām iṉṟu namatu nāṭṭup pōrk karuvikaḷip paṟṟi ārāya namatu tolkāppiyam tirukkuṟaḷ nikaṇṭukaḷ kaliṅkattup paraṇi akanāṉūṟu puṟanāṉūṟu cilappatikāram maṇimēkali cīvaka cintāmaṇi pattup pāṭṭu mutaliya nūṟkaḷi ārāyntu pārkka vēṇṭiyatirukkiṟatu.  inta nūṟkaḷ pōrkkāviyaṅkaḷāka illā viṭṭālum paḻam perum iccaṅkakāla nūṟkaḷilum ataṟkup piṉ eḻunta nūṟkaḷilum cila paṭikkalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  cila nūṟkaḷil caṅka kāla maṉṉarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya oru cila pōrkkaruvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  cilavaṟṟil makaḷirkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷum nutalkaḷum puruvaṅkaḷum akkālattil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vanta pōrkkaruvikaḷukku oppāka iruntaṉa eṉṟu upamāṉamāka eṭuttāḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivikaḷik koṇṭu antak kāppiyam allatu tōttirap pāṭalkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa kālattil atil kuṟippiṭṭa āyutaṅkaḷ iruntaṉavākak karutalām.
āṉāl makāpāratak katikaḷi vittu vaṭa intiyāvil akkālattil atil kuṟippiṭum āyutaṅkaḷ iruntatākak karuta muṭiyum.  atu tamiḻil ākkappaṭṭatik koṇṭu tamiḻakattil irunta āyutamāka eṇṇa eḷḷaḷavum iṭamilli.  āṉāl vāṉmīki eḻutiya vaṭamoḻi irāmāyaṇatti eṭuttuk koṇṭālum kampaṉ eḻutiya vāṉmīkiyit taḻuvi eḻutiya irāma kāti eṭuttuk koṇṭālum atil kāṇappaṭṭa āyutaṅkaḷ aṉittum tamiḻarkaḷ āyutaṅkaḷ eṉṟu tiriyamākak kūṟamuṭiyum.  atē pōl peruṅkati tamiḻil ākkappaṭṭiruntālum, atil kūṟappaṭum āyutaṅkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu āyutaṅkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇamuṭiyum.
tolkāppiyam
tolkāppiyam iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tamiḻakattil eḻunta paḻam perum ilakkaṇa nūl eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu.  itu ilakkaṇa nūl eṉṟālum poruḷ atikārattil akkālattil tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē nilaviya teyva vaḻipāṭukaḷ, makkaḷ vāḻnta nilaṅkaḷ, avarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟi, nāṭu, aracu, vaṇikam, nāṭṭuk kāval, oḻukkam, kaṭal vaṇikam, talivaṉ vaṇikattiṉ poruṭṭu taliviyiviṭṭup pirintu kaṭal kaṭantu kappalēṟi piṟa nāṭu ceṉṟu mīṇṭum taliviyiṭam vantu cērvatu pōṉṟa ceytikaḷiyellām eṭuttuk kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
āciriyar tolkāppiyar kālattilē tamiḻakattil camūka nili perum māṟṟattiṟkuḷḷāki viṭṭatu.  tamiḻakattil tāy āṭci māṟi tanti āṭci eḻuntu appāl atuvum māṟi maṉṉaṉ āṭci eḻuntu viṭṭatu.  maṉṉarkaḷ palar tōṉṟi oruvarukkoruvar pōriṭṭum vantaṉar.  muṭi maṉṉarkaḷum kuṟu nila maṉṉarkaḷum nilakkiḻārkaḷum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉar.  maṉṉarkaḷ oruvarōṭoruvar palvēṟu kāriyaṅkaḷukkākap pōriṭṭu vantaṉar.
akkālattē tamiḻ aracarkaḷ tāṉip  paṭi (kālāṭ paṭi) yāṉippaṭi, kutirippaṭi, tērppaṭi eṉum nāl vakip paṭikaḷiyum amittu ataṉ mūlam pōriṭṭu vantaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu.1
tolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷ mūlam tamiḻarkaḷ kiṟittava ūḻi piṟappataṟkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē karippaṭi, parip paṭi, tērppaṭi, kālāṭpaṭi pōṉṟa nālvakip paṭikaḷi yum peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  tērilē ēṟi vanta porunar mutaliyōr veṟṟi īṭṭiya vēl vēntarkaḷip pukaḻntu kūṟiya ceytikaḷ kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.  tērēṟip pōr ceyya vanta vēntarkaḷi veṟṟi īṭṭiya vēntaṉ, taṉ  veṟṟik kaḷippāl tērttaṭṭilē niṉṟu kaḷikoṇṭu kuravik kūttu āṭiyatum atikkaṇṭu avaṉatu pōr vīrarkaḷ kaḷinaṭaṉam āṭuvatum kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟatu.  tērppaṭikaḷiṉ ciṟappu peritum pōṟṟap paṭukiṟatu.  āṉāl tolkāppiyar eḻutiya toṉmiyāṉa ilakkiyamākiya tolkāppiyam muḻuvatum turuvit turuvip pārttum akkāla makkaḷ kaṭaṟpōr āṟṟiya ceyti tuḷi kūṭak kāṇa muṭiyavilli.  kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟukaḷum kiṭṭavilli.  tolkāppiyar kālattil pōr naṭantatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  pōr muṟikaḷ vakukkappaṭṭiruntatu.  nāṉku ēṉ? aintu vitamāṉa paṭikaḷum, paṭittalivarkaḷum iruntaṉar eṉṟum naṉku terikiṟatu.  tamiḻar kaṇṭa pōr muṟikaḷ tolkāppiyap puṟattiṇi iyalilē naṉku terikiṟatu.  pōril vil, ampu, vēl, vāḷ, kiṭuku, kēṭayam pōṉṟa tātup poruḷkaḷiṉālāṉa pōrkkaruvikaḷ pala iruntaṉa eṉṟum terikiṟatu.2
tirukkuṟaḷ
tirukkuṟaḷ nīti nūl eṉṟālum atil uḷḷa muppālil oṉṟāṉa poruṭpālil paṭi kuṭi, kūḻ, amiccu, araṇ eṉṟa āṟu aṅkaṅkaḷ viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟatu.  ceṅkōṉmi, koṭuṅ kōṉmi, oṟṟāṭal, tūtu, nāṭu, paṭimāṭci, paṭic cerukku mutaliyavikaḷip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭuvatōṭu pala iṭaṅkaḷil akkālattil irunta āyutaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  pōr muṟikaḷum pōrt tantiraṅkaḷum eṭuttuk kāṭṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tiruvaḷḷuvar kālam kiṟittava ūḻiyi oṭṭi eḻunta kālam.  atāvatu iṟṟikku pattoṉpatu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ uḷḷa kālam.  kuṟaḷil kūṟappaṭum āyutaṅkaḷ cumār iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku  muṉpē tamiḻarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttivanta āyutam eṉṟu tiriyamākak kūṟa muṭiyum.
vaḷḷuvar muṟkālattil tamiḻakattil irunta araṇkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟum poḻutu uyaram akalam uṟuti pakivarāl aḻikka muṭiyāta arumi ākiya inta nāṉkum amintiruntati tam kuṟaḷil aḻakāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
uyarvakalam tiṇmi arumiyin nāṉkiṉ
amivaraṇ eṉṟurikkum nūl 	- tirukkuṟaḷ 743
iḵtaṉṟi akkālattil tamiḻakattil tamiḻ makkaḷ vil1, kaṇi2, vēl3, vāḷ4 mutaliya karuvikaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉpati naṉkaṟikiṉṟōm.  inta āyutaṅkaḷip palaviṭaṅkaḷil uvamāṉamāka eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟār.  pala viṭaṅkaḷil pōr niliyilum eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟār.
maṟṟiru iṭaṅkaḷil kivēl5 aṅkucam6 ākiya karuvikaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  mēlum vaḷḷuvar kālattil tamiḻ nāṭṭil tāṉi, yāṉi, kutiri, tēr ākiya nālvakip paṭikaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟi periya nāvāykaḷum1 iruntaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
vaḷḷuvar kālattil tamiḻnāṭu palvēṟu tuṟikaḷilum muṉṉēṟiyiruntatu.  nālvakip paṭiyuṭaṉ kaṭaṟpaṭiyum ciṟappuṟa amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  tōṇi, puṉi, nāvāy, vaṅkam, kappal, pōrkkappalkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa uruvākkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  nāṭṭil palvēṟu araṇkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.  tolkāppiyar kālattilē araṇkaḷum muṟṟukiyiṭṭup pōrpuriyum muṟiyum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.  eṉavē vaḷḷuvar kālattil vil, vēl, vāḷ, kiṭuku, kēṭayam maṭṭumiṉṟi palvēṟu vitamāṉa vilkaḷum ampu
kaḷum vāḷ vakikaḷum vēl vakikaḷum īṭṭi vakikaḷum maḻuvaki
kaḷum tōṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu ūkikka iṭamuṇṭu.  vaḷḷuvar kālattiṟku muṉpē kati, taṇṭam, cakkaram, (tikari) vēka pācam tiricūlam, maḻu, paracu, kuttuvāḷ, kikaḷ eṟivēl, pūmarāṅ, kūṉvāḷ koṭuvāḷ, arivāḷ, kōṭari, (navir) īrvāḷ uṭivāḷ, nākapācam kuntam kūntālam, vacciram, cammaṭṭi ulakki mutaliya eṇṇaṟṟa āyutaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
cilappatikāram
cilappatikāram iṟṟikku 18- nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ eḻunta nūl eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  innūl eḻunta kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ aḷapperum ariya nākarikattip paṭitta iṉattavarkaḷāy vāḻa muṟpaṭṭu viṭṭārkaḷ.  cilappatikārattil tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa āṭaṟkaliyiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum, icik karuvikaḷiyum āṭi vakikaḷiyum āyntu pārkkum poḻutu tamiḻarkaḷ aintu vitamāṉa paṭikaḷi amittu ellā vitamāṉa pōrk karuvi
kaḷiyum peṟṟu kumari mutal imayam vari ceṉṟu veṉṟu veṟṟik koṭiyi imayattiṉ mītu paṭṭoḷi vīcip paṟakkum paṭi ceytu vantuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  īḻam, kāḻakam, cāvakam, puṭpakam mutaliya kaṭal kaṭantuḷḷa nāṭukaḷiyellām veṉṟu eṅkum taṅkaḷ kayal, puli, vil, nanti mutaliya ciṉṉaṅkaḷ poṟitta koṭiyi nili nāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpataṟku eṇṇaṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭa muṭiyum.  tamiḻakattil, paṇṭu eḻunta kaṭaṟ
kōḷik kuṟippiṭum poḻutu,
vaṭivēl eṟinta vāṉ paki poṟātu- cilap. maturi 11. 18 
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatiliruntu muṟkālattilē tamiḻarkaḷ vēl eṉpati aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
vaḻaṅku vil taṭakki maṟakkuṭit tāyattu  
						- cila. vēṭṭu 12:6
eṉṉum cilappatikārac ceyyuḷ, vil eṉṉum paḻam perum tamiḻar āyutatti eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu.  koṟṟaviyip paṟṟik kūṟum poḻutu avaḷ aṇintuḷḷa cūlam, vāḷ mūṅkilāl ceyta vil, cakkaram mutaliya karuvikaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
vaḷiyuṭik kiyiṟ cūlam ēnti     - cilap. vēṭṭu. 12:60
aiyi ceyyavaḷ veyyavāḷ taṭakki 
		- cilap. vēṭṭu 12:69
vēyvil eyiṉar kulaṉē kulaṉum
caṅkamum cakkaram		        -    cilap. vēṭṭu. 12:19
cilappatikāram maturikkāṇṭam ūrkāṇkātiyil maturik kaṭi vītiyil uḷḷa cila poruḷkaḷik kūṟu miṭattu meypuku kavacam, maṇikaḷ patitta tōṭṭiyum aṅkucamum, tōlāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kittaḷamum, arippaṭṭikiyum, vaḷi taṭiyum, paṉṟi mukak kaṭakamum, kiṭukiṉ paṭamum, kuttuk kōlkaḷum īrvāḷ mutaliya ulōkattāṟ ceyta karuvikaḷum kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
meypuku kavacamum vīḻmaṇit tōṭṭiyum
ataḷ puṉi araṇamum ariyā yōkamum
vaḷitaru kuḻiyamum vāl veṇ kavariyum
ēṉappaṭamum kiṭukiṉ paṭamum
kāṉappaṭamum kāmūṉṟu kaṭikiyum
cempiṟ ceytavum kañcat toḻilavum 
	- cilap. maturi ūr 14: 169-470
cilappatikāram maturik kāṇṭattil aṭikkalak kāti yiṉ iṟutiyil maturi mānakariṉ nila varaṇil poṟikaḷ amikkap peṟṟiruntatu.  avikaḷāvaṉa:  (1) vaḷiviṟ poṟi (2) karuviralūkam (3) kallumiḻ kavaṇ (4) parivuṟu ven ney (5) pākaṭu kuḻici (6) kāya poṉṉuli (7)kalliṭu kūṭi (8) tūṇṭil (9) toṭakku 
(10) āṇṭaliyaṭuppu (11) kavi (12) kaḻu (13)puti (14) puḻi 
(15) aiyavittulām (16) kipeyarūci  (17) ceṉṟari ciral (18) paṉṟi (19) paṇi (20) eḻuvuñcīppu (21) kaṇiyam (22) kōl (23) muntam (24) vēl (25) ñāyil (26) nūṟṟavarik kolli (27) taḷḷi veṭṭi (28) kaḷiṟṟup poṟi (29) viḻuṅkum pāmpu (30) kaḻukup poṟi 
(31) pulip poṟi (32) kuṭap pāmpu (33) cakaṭap poṟi (34) takarp poṟi (35) aṟinūṟpoṟi pōṉṟavikaḷākum.

kōlum kuntamum vēlum piṟavum
ñāyiṟum ciṟantu nāṭkoṭi nuṭaṅkum 
	- cilap. matu. aṭi 153: 207 - 217
iṉṉum, cilappatikārattil pala viṭaṅkaḷil kōṭari (naviyam) īṭṭi (vēl) pōṉṟa cila āyutaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  āṉāl, avinayaṅkaḷ, muracukaḷ, aṇikalaṉ kaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷ virivāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  avikaḷip pōl pōrkkaruvikaḷ iṅku virivākak kūṟappaṭa villi.  kāraṇam itu pōrkkāviyam alla.  atōṭu iṅku pōr nilikaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭum campavaṅkaḷum iṅku iṭam peṟavilli.  uriyāciriyarkaḷukkuk kūṭa intac cantarppam kiṭiyāmaṟ pōṉatu namatu turatirṭṭam eṉṟutāṉ kūṟa vēṇṭum.  ilakkiyam pōr ilakkiyamāka maṭṭum amintu viṭṭāl pōtātu. nūl āciriyaṉ aracaṉākavō allatu paṭit tali
vaṉāka iruntāl tāṉ pōr muṟikaḷum, pōrk karuvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum atip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟikaḷum teḷivāy nūlil iṭam peṟum.
pōr ilakkiyam oṉṟu tamiḻk karuvūlam pōṉṟu eḻun tuḷḷatu.  anta ariya pōr ilakkiyamākiya kaliṅkattup paraṇiyip pārttu nāṉ perum ēmāṟṟam aṭintēṉ.  kaliṅkattupparaṇi pōr ilakkiyamāka maṭṭumiṉṟi 11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil eḻunta piṟkāla ilakkiyamākavum irukkiṟatu.  atōṭu cōḻap pēraracu nalla poruḷātāra vaḷamum, nākarikamum, aṟivum, āṟṟalum peṟṟu intiyat tuṇik kaṇṭattiyē taṉ paṭi valiyiṉāl veṉṟu āḻkaṭal kaṭantu āyiram kal toliviṟku appāl uḷḷa pala kiḻakkāciya nāṭukaḷi yellām aṭipaṇiya vittu veṟṟi koṇṭa kālattil eṇṇaṟṟa pōrk karuvikaḷi yellām paṭittu avaṉi mecca vīṟṟirunta kālattil eḻutap peṟṟa ilakkiyamākum.  inta nūliṉ uriyācirikaḷ kūṭa akkālattup pōrkkaruvikaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭavilli.
kaliṅkattupparaṇi
kaliṅkattupparaṇi eṉṉum nūl mutalām kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ kaliṅka nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttu veṟṟi yīṭṭiyatic ciṟappittu pāṭiya nūlākum.  innūlil pala pōrk karuvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  intap pōril payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭa āyutaṅkaḷ aṉittum tamiḻarkaḷāl ki.pi 12-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ ārampak kālattil (ki.pi. 1112-il) payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatākum. 
kaliṅkattup paraṇiyil 12-ām attiyāyam pōr pāṭiyatu eṉpatākum.  atil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷa āyutaṅkaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟu:
aṭu cili pakaḻito ṭuttuvi ṭappukum
aḷiviṉi layametir viṭṭavar veṭṭiṉa  	- kaliṅ 421
eṉṉum ceyyuḷil cili (vil) pakaḻi (kaṇi) ākiyavikaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṭṭa vaṭṭaṇaṅkaḷ mēleṟinta vēl 	- kaliṅ 426
eṉṉum ceyyuḷil (vaṭṭaṇaṅkaḷ) kēṭayaṅkaḷ, vēl eṉṟa iru karuvikaḷ pōril payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
ulakki yucci tittapōtu muṅkalappi yokkumē  
			- kaliṅ 427
kaḷitta vīrar viṭṭa nēmi kaṇṭu vicu taṇṭici 	  
		- kaliṅ 432
eṉṉum ceyyuṭkaḷil ulakki, nēmi (cakkarāyutaṅkaḷ) taṇṭāyu taṅkaḷ (taṇṭu) payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
vāḷil veṭṭi vāraṇatti tōḷiliṭṭa mintarkaḷ 
			- kaliṅ 435
aciyavurat taḻuttiya yivuḷiyi
aṭucavaḷattu eṭutta poḻutavi  	- kaliṅ 437
kurutik kuṭṭa mittaṉiyuṅ kōlum, vēlum, 								kuntamumē    - kaliṅ 508 
eṉṉum ceyyuṭkaḷil vāḷ, īṭṭi, vil, vēl, kuntamum (oruvaki īṭṭiyum) kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
uruviya curikiyoṭu uyarkaṇi virupaṭi 	- kaliṅ 589
eṉṉum ceyyuḷil uṭivāḷ (curiki) eṉṟa pōrkkaruvi kūṟap paṭukiṟatu.
iḵtaṉṟi innūlil atikamāṉa pōrkkaruvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ etuvum kāṇappaṭavilli.  innūl paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil mikavum ciṟappu vāynta cōḻar perumpaṭi naṭattiya māperum pōr āṉatiṉāl iṅku atikamāṉa pōrk karuvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ etirppārkkak kūṭiyatēyākum.  uri yāciriyarkaḷ kūṭa atika
māṉa pōr karuvikaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭat tavaṟiviṭṭārkaḷ.
puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli
puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli puṟapporuḷiyē poruḷāka uṭiyatu.  itiluḷḷa pākkaḷ pala iḷampūraṇar, parimēlaḻakar aṭiyārkku nallār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliya āciriyarkaḷāl mēṟkōḷāka eṭuttāḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
innūlil paṇṭikkāla maṉṉarkaḷiṉ pōr muṟiyum, vīrarkaḷiṉ tiṟaṉum, āyutaṅkaḷiṉ ciṟappum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
veḷvāṭ karuṅkaḻaṟkāl veñcuṭar vēl ṟaṇṇaḷiyāṉ
koḷvāṉ koṭittāṉi koṇṭeḻuntāṉ - naḷḷātār
añcuvaru vāyi larumiḷik kuṇṭakaḻi
mañcivaru ñāyiṉ matil  		(pu.ve.mā.99)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṟku, oḷi paranta vāḷiṉiyum valiya vīrak kaḻalāṟ ciṟanta kāliṉiyum veyya cōtiyāṟ polinta vēliṉiyum taṇṇaḷiyiyum uṭiyavaṉ kippaṟṟik koḷvāṉākak koṭiyiyuṭiya paṭiyi oṉṟupaṭak koṇṭeḻuntāṉ.  poruntātār veruvaru vāyiṉiyum pukutaṟkariya kuṟuṅkāṭṭi ṉiyum āḻnta kiṭaṅkiṉiyum (akaḻiyiyum) mēkam nilavum ēval aṟiyiṉiyuṭiya kōṭṭiyi eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.  itil vāḷ, vēl ākiya āyutaṅkaḷum araṇum kuṟip piṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
vil muṉ kaṇi teriyum vēṭṭicciṟār 
		- (pu.ve.mā.177)
eṉṟa ceyyuḷil villum kaṇiyum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
nāvāyum tōṇiyu mēl koṇṭu naḷḷātār
ōvār vilaṅki yuṭalavum    	- (pu.ve. mā 181)
eṉṟa ceyyuḷil nāvāy tōṇi pōṉṟa kalaṉkaḷ kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iyavar pukaḻa veṟimura cārppak
kuyavari vēṅki yaṉi - vayavar
eṉṉum pāṭṭil vaḷivāṉa arivāḷāṉa kuyavari kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.  atip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟikaḷum avikaḷiṉ valivum pakivarkaḷ antak karuvikaḷiṉ tākkutalkaḷi eppaṭi camāḷit tārkaḷ eṉpavip paṟṟiyum viḷakkam kāṇappaṭum.  iṉṟu kiṭikkum tamiḻ nūṟkaḷil attakiya vivaraṅkaḷ etuvum kāṇa muṭiyavilli.  potuvāka ellā nūṟkaḷilum, periya pāṟikaḷ kūrmiyāṉa kaṟkaḷ, kati, taṭi, ulakki, katti, vēl, īṭṭi, vāḷ, kōṭari, pācam, vil, ampu, poṟikaḷ mutaliyavikaḷē kāṇap
paṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
irāmāyaṇam
vāṉmīkiyiṉ vaṭamoḻi irāmāyaṇattil payaṉpaṭuttiya pōrkkaruvikaḷ ulakkikaḷ,  irumpulakkikaḷ, taṭikaḷ katikaḷ, uḻalittaṭikaḷ, irumputtaṭikaḷ, irumpup pūṇ kaṭṭiya taṭikaḷ, kattikaḷ, paṭṭākkattikaḷ, arivāḷ, vēl vakikaḷ tōmaraṅkaḷ palavaki īṭṭikaḷ, pērīṭṭikaḷ, palavakik kōṭarikaḷ, vacciraṅ kaḷ (caktikaḷ), kūrāṉa kōṭāri, palavakic cūlaṅkaḷ, pācak kayiṟukaḷ, nākapācam, periya cammaṭṭikaḷ - koḻukkaḷ paṭṭacaṅkaḷ, miṉṉuñ cakkaraṅkaḷ, ciṟiya cakkaraṅkaḷ, periya  cakkaraṅkaḷ, vēlāyutaṅkaḷ, piṇṭipālaṅkaḷ, catakiṉikaḷ, pirācam, mutkaram, palavakiyāṉa vilkaḷ, pirāmātiram, aknēyātiram, jaṣikaatiram, aintirātiram, karṇiyātiram aṣṭi ampukaḷ, kattippāṇam, artta cantira pāṇam, cūriya mukappāṇam, carppamukap pāṇam, mutalimukap pāṇam, aintutali nākap pāṇam, kaḻuti mukappāṇam pōṉṟa eṇṇilāp pāṇa vakikaḷum atiram catiram eṉṉum pērāl koṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ivikaḷ vevvēṟu nāḷil vevvēṟu āṭkaḷāl payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  oruvaṉ nākātiratti viṭuttāl maṟṟavaṉ atit taṭukka karuṭātiratti viṭuttu atit taṭuttāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 

5. ciṟpa nūṟ cāṉṟukaḷ 
tiruppam
ātikālattil namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ uṇavip peṟuvataṟku alintu tirintu vantaṉar.  appāl uṇavi uṟpatti ceyya muṟpaṭṭaṉar; uṇavi uṟpatti ceyvataṟku ēṟṟa nilattik kaṇṭaṉar; uḻukaruvikaḷi uruvākkiṉar.  uṇavup poruḷkaḷi vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavikaḷum aḻittu viṭāmal taṭuppataṟku villiyum vēliyum, vāḷiyum ceytaṉar.
piṉṉar vil, vēl, vāḷ, mutaliya karuvikaḷi apivirutti ceytum aḻakupaṭuttiyum kūriyatākkiyum vantaṉar.  ayalavarkaḷ uṇavup poruḷkaḷik kavarntu koṇṭu pōyviṭātu taṭuttu vantaṉar.  uḻukaruvikaḷē piṟkālattil paṭikkalaṉkaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻakattil 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil kumpiṉi yār āṭci tōṉṟumvari paḻiya paṭikkalaṉkaḷē payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭu vantaṉa.  iṟutiyākat tamiḻakamum intiyāvum āṅkila āṭciyiṉarāl aṭimippaṭuttappaṭṭatu.  aṭimi vāḻkkiyil makkaḷukkuc cuvi ūṭṭap peṟṟatu.  makkaḷiṉ kaliyum kalviyum karuvaṟukkappaṭṭatu.  makkaḷiṉ pōrk kuṇamum vīramum kuṉṟiyatu.  āṭciyāḷarkaḷiṉ āṅkilak kalvi muṟi aṭimi vāḻkki  eḻum paṭiyāyc ceytu viṭṭatu.
avarkaḷ mutalil namatu paṭikkalaṉkaḷi aḻittaṉar.  avikaḷik kāṇpataṟkē aritākac ceytu viṭṭaṉar.  āṅkila āṭciyiṉ pōtu eḻunta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ellām kātal ilakkiyaṅ
kaḷāka iruntaṉavē yoḻiya pōr ilakkiyaṅkaḷāka etuvum illi.  kuṟippākat tamiḻ makkaḷiṭam pōrkkuṇam poṉṟum
paṭi ceytu viṭṭaṉar.
tāyt teyvam
namatu ātit teyvam kāḷi.  avaḷ paḻiyōḷ, karuppi, māyōḷ, pattira kāḷi, koṟṟavi eṉṟellām aḻikkap paṭṭār.  avaḷiṉ 5000 -m āṇṭiṟku muṉṉuḷḷa uruvam arappāvil kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  ātiyil avaḷ irukikaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappeṟṟālum piṉ pattira kāḷiyāy patiṉāṟu kikaḷ peṟṟu pala āyutaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟāḷ.  avaḷ nirvāṇiyāy kāṇappaṭuvatu avaḷ paḻam perum teyvam eṉpati uṟutippaṭuttukiṟatu.  avaḷatu kaṇavaṉākiya civa perumāṉ tōlāṭi aṇintiruppatiṉāl avaḷukku piṉ - tamiḻarkaḷ pañcāṭiyi aṟiyumuṉ eḻunta teyvamākat tikaḻkiṟār.  ātiyil uruvaṅkaḷ ceyyumuṉ tamiḻarkaḷ kal vil vēl, cūlam kati mutaliyavikaḷi vittē teyva vaḻipāṭu ceytu vantaṉar.1
tamiḻarkaḷ vāḻnta aintu nilaṅkaḷilum  kāḷiyē teyvamāka vaḻipaṭappaṭṭāḷ.  aṉṟu aintu nilaṅkaḷilum tāy āṭciyē nilaviyatu.  tāy āṭci māṟiyatum tāyt teyva vaḻipāṭum kuṉṟiyatu.  āṉāl nīṇṭakālam pāli nilattil koṟṟaviyē vaḻipaṭu teyvamāka matikkappaṭṭāḷ.  iṉṟum pāli nila makkaḷ kāḷi vaḻipāṭu naṭatti varukiṟārkaḷ.  tāy vīrattiṉ ciṉṉamāka aṉṟu iruntāḷ.  vīramikka maṟavarkaḷ pāli nilattil koṟṟa viyi viṭātu pōṟṟi vantaṉar.  mulli nilattilum ātiyil karumi niṟam vāynta māyōḷ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭu vantāḷ.  uruvam ākkum kaliyi makkaḷ aṟivataṟku muṉ avaḷatu kiyilirunta katiyi vittu nīrvārttu malar cūṭṭi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.  appāl ammi āṭci akaṉṟu aṇṇaṉ āṭci arumpum poḻutu māyōḷ eṉṉum patam māyōṉ ākat tiripuṟṟu mulli nilattil aṇṇaṉ (tampi) āṭciyum māyōṉ ākiya āṇ teyva vaḻipāṭum eḻuntatu.  iti civa camaya aṟiñarkaḷum ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.2
māyōḷ cakkarāyutaḷ ākavum katiyik kiyil ēntiyava ḷākavum pōṟṟappaṭṭāḷ.  katiyum, piṉṉar cakkaramum aṇṇaṉ teyvattiṉ ciṉṉamāka vaḻipaṭappaṭṭu vantatu.
kuṟiñci nilattil tāy āṭciyum tāyt teyvamum nilaviya poḻutu tāyiṉ kiyilirunta vēlit tāyt teyvamāka eṇṇi nīrvārttu malar puṉintu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu.  appāl tāy āṭci akaṟṟappaṭṭut taṉayaṉ āṭci eḻuntatum avaṉ tāyiṉ kiyi
lirunta vēlit taṉ urimip poruḷākak koṇṭāṉ.  mēlum avaṉ vēṭṭiyāṭit taṉ tāy, taṅki, akki, aṇṇaṉ, tampi ākiya uṟaviṉarkaḷik kāppāṟṟavum pakivar kaḷik koṉṟu aḻittu taṉṉiyum taṉ uṟaviṉarkaḷiyum pātukākkum āyuta
māka vēl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.  makkaḷ vēlit tāṅkiya makaṉ teyvattiṉ ciṉṉamāka avaṉ kiyil uḷḷa vēli vaḻi
paṭṭatuṭaṉ avaṉi vēlaṉ eṉṟu kūṟi vaṇaṅki vantaṉar.  aṉṟu tantiyum tantit teyvamum tōṉṟa villi.  mēlum tanti
yiṉ uṭimi taṉayaṉukku urittu eṉṟa camūka amippum eḻavilli.  eṉavē vēlaṉ tantiyiṉ peyarāl aḻikkap peṟātu atāvatu civaṉār makaṉ eṉṟu aḻikkap peṟātu koṟṟavi makaṉ eṉṟum paḻiyōaḷ kuḻavi eṉṟum aḻikkap
paṭṭāṉ.1  appāl vēli uṭiyavaṉātalāl vēlaṉ eṉṟum piṉṉar murukaṉ eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭāṉ.
itē pōṉṟu ātiyil maruta nilattilum koṟṟaviyē vaḻipaṭu teyvamāka eṇṇi vaṇaṅkappaṭṭāḷ.  uruvam ceyyum kali eḻumuṉ avaḷatu mūvili vēlik koṟṟaviyāka eṇṇi vaḻipaṭappaṭṭu vantatu.  avaḷukku mūvili vēṟkumari eṉṟu peyar(nikaṇṭu). aṉṟu maruta nilattil tāy āṭciyum tāyt teyvamum iruntu vantatu.  appāl tāy āṭci māṟi vēntaṉ āṭci eḻuntatu.  tāyt teyvattiṟkup patilākat tantit teyvam eḻuntatu.  camūkattil kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyāka vāḻum camūka muṟiyum kaṭṭuppāṭum oḻukkamum piṟantaṉa. koṟṟavikku, cūlikkup patilāka avaḷatu kaṇavaṉākiya cūlapāṇiyākiya civa perumāṉ vaḻipāṭu eḻuntatu.  civaṉ uruvam ceyyum ciṟpak kali tōṉṟāta tāl civaṉuṭiya ciṉṉamākiya tiricūlam vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu.  civaperumāṉ ātiyil taṉ maṉiviyākiya koṟṟaviyiṭatti ṉiṉṟu peṟṟa mukkaṇṇiyum cūlattiyum peṟṟa teyvamākavum paracu, mūvili vēl, vil pōṉṟa āyutaṅkaḷit tāṅkip pakivarkaḷi aḻittum kāṭukaḷi veṭṭiyum nilatti uḻutum māṭukaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vanta vēntaṉ niliyil eḻunta teyva
mākavum eṇṇi vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār.  ivaṉ mukkaṇṇaṉ cūlapāṇi.  ivaṉ pulittōli āṭiyāka uṭutti yāṉit tōli pōrviyāka aṇintiruppatu oṉṟē ivaṉ kaṟkālat teyvam eṉpatip pulappaṭuttum.
muṉṉiya nāṉilaṅkaḷilum nikaḻntatip pōl neytal nilattilum tāy āṭciyum tāyt teyvamum avaḷatu āyutamākiya cuṟāmīṉ kompum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭu vantu piṉ tāy āṭci māṟi āṇ āṭci arumpum poḻutu vāraṇaṉ eṉṉum āṇ teyva vaḻipāṭum avaṉatu āyutamākiya cuṟāmīṉ kompi nāṭṭi vaḻipaṭum vaḻakkamum eḻuntatu.
potuvāka, cumār āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ ellāp pakutikaḷilum kuṭumpattalivi allatu kuṭumpattalivaṉ āṭci yum tāyt teyvam (māyōḷ) vaṇakkamum nilavi appāl māyōṉ (aṇṇaṉ) vaḻipāṭum vēlaṉ vaḻipāṭum, mukkaṇṇaṉ vaḻipāṭum vāraṇaṉ (varuṇaṉ) vaḻipāṭum nilaviyatu.  inta aintu nilaṅkaḷukkuriya periya teyvaṅkaḷ aṉittum pōrt tey vaṅkaḷākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.  ivarkaḷ aṉivarum iraṇṭu nāṉku, āṟu, eṭṭu patiṉāṟu pōṉṟavikaḷiyuṭiyavarkaḷāy ovvoru kiyilum vevvēṟu āyutaṅkaḷit tāṅkiyavar
kaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.  ivarkaḷatu kikaḷil uḷḷa āyutaṅkaḷē tamiḻakattil cumār 3000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa āyutaṅkaḷāka eṇṇalām.  tamiḻar kaṇṭa aintu nilattilum aintu pōrk karuvikaḷ teyvamāka vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭu vantaṉa.
namatu nāṭṭil uḷḷa perun teyvaṅkaḷit tavira ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷum pala uḷa.  avarkaḷ kikaḷilum palvēṟu vitamāṉa āyutaṅkaḷ uḷa.  ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷum perun teyvaṅkaḷum taṅkaḷuk kum taṅkaḷ uṟaviṉarkaḷukkum paktarkaḷukkum virōtamāka irup pavarkaḷuṭaṉ taṅkaḷiṉ āyutaṅkaḷik koṇṭu pōrāṭiya ceyti
kaḷ purāṇaṅkaḷil viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  it teyvaṅkaḷ camūka vaḷarcciyil atikamāṉa āyutaṅkaḷiyum atikamāṉa kikaḷi yum peṟṟum irukkiṉṟaṉa.  paktarkaḷum ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷum periya teyvaṅkaḷip pala āṇṭukaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu avarkaḷiṭamiruntu pala ariya āyutaṅkaḷip peṟṟa katikaḷumuṇṭu.  makkaḷiṉ camūka vaḷarcciyum aṟivu vaḷarcciyum it teyvaṅkaḷiṉ kikaḷil āyutaṅkaḷāyp pariṇamittiruntatip purāṇaṅkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉa.  makkaḷ nuṇṇiya aṟivukaḷip peṟṟa kālattil teyvaṅ kaḷ āyutaṅkaḷip peṟāmal pakivarkaḷi vāyāl capikkiṉṟaṉar.  neṟṟik kaṇṇāl erikkiṉṟaṉar. puṉ cirippāl aḻittuviṭum āṟṟalip peṟṟuḷḷaṉar.
eṉavē purāṇaṅkaḷiyum, ciṟpa nūlkaḷiyum ārāyntu pārttāl teyvaṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya āyutaṅkaḷ ivi yeṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭittu viṭalām.  avi paḻaṅkaṟkālattiliruntu ki.pi.1500-vari irunta namatu nāṭṭu āyutaṅkaḷākak karutalām.  ataṟkup piṉ namatu nāṭṭil putut teyvaṅkaḷ eḻuntatākat teriyavilli.  oru vēḷi cērmaṉ cāmi, ciṅkaḷatti ammāṉ, paṭṭāṇi, karuppaṉ māṭaṉ, muṇṭaṉ mutaliya cila teyvaṅkaḷ eḻuntālum avarkaḷukkup paḻiya teyvaṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya āyutaṅkaḷ koṭukkappaṭavē illi.
kāḷiyiṉ āyutam
vaṭa intiyāvil uḷḷa turkki allatu caṇṭi pattu mutal patiṉāṟu kikaḷiyuṭiyavaḷāy irukkiṉṟāḷ.1  namatu nāṭṭu pattirakāḷi 16 kikaḷiyuṭiyavaḷāyt tikaḻkiṉṟāḷ.  eṉavē avaḷ pōrt teyvamākavum kākkum teyvamākavum iruppatāl avaḷ patiṉāṟu āyutaṅkaḷukkuk kuṟiyātu peṟṟirukkalām.  kāḷi eṉṟa peyaruṭiya teyvam irukikaḷiyum palakikaḷi yum makākāḷi 4 kikaḷiyum piṟa kāḷikaḷ pala kikaḷiyum peṟ ṟirukkalām.
kāḷiyiṉ āyutaṅkaḷ; 1 kēṭayam, 2 paracu, 3 kati, 4 pācam, 
5 vil, 6 cakkaram, 7 cakti, 8 ampu, 9 ulakki, 10 cūlam, 11 katti, 
12 nākapācam, 13 vāḷ, 14 cakti (vēl), 15 aṅkucam, 16 vacciram, 
17 kēṭayam, 18 kalappi, 19 tiricūlam pōṉṟa āyutaṅkaḷākum.
civaperumāṉiṉ āyutam
ivaḷatu kaṇavaṉāṉa civaperumāṉ iṉṉum atikamāṉa āyutaṅkaḷi uṭiyavarākat tikaḻkiṟār.  kāraṇam avaratu conta āyutaṅkaḷōṭu taṉatu maṉiviyākiya koṟṟaviyiṉ āyutam cērntatēyākum.
1. paracu (maḻu), 2. cūlam, 3. tiricūlam, 4. caṅku, 5. aṅkucam, 6. vil, 7. pācam (kayiṟu), 8. kēṭayam, 9. kati, 10. yāṉik kompu, 11. katti, 12.vacciram, 13. kaṭvāṅkam, 14.. ampu, 15. kalappi, 16. vāḷ, 17. īṭṭi, 18. ulakki, 19. nākapācam, 20. eṟiyum katti, 21. taṇṭam, 22. tī, 
23. kūntāli.
māyōṉ āyutam
māyōḷiṉ (kāḷiyiṉ) tamiyaṉākiya māyōṉ taṅki peṟṟirunta āyutaṅkaḷiviṭac ciṟitu kuṟivākap peṟṟirukkiṟār.  ataṉ vivaramāvatu.
1. caṅku, 2. cakkaram,  3. vāḷ, 4. kati, 5. ulakki, 6. pācam,  7. kēṭayam, 8. kalappi, 9. vacciram, 10. maḻu, 11. vil, 12. ampu, 13. īṭṭi, 14. cūlam (vēl) ākiyavikaḷēyākum.
mulli nilat teyvamākiya māyōṉ, māyōḷiṉ aṇṇaṉ ākiyāl ivar koṟṟaviyiṉ āyutaṅkaḷiyē peritum tāṅki irukkiṟār.1
vēlaṉ āyutam
vēlaṉ allatu murukaṉ koṟṟaviyiṉ makaṉ āvaṉ.  piṟa teyvaṅkaḷiviṭak kuṟivāṉa āyutaṅkaḷiyē peṟṟirukkiṟāṉ.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
(1) vēl (2) ampu(pāṇam) (3) vil (4) vacciram (5) katti (6) cakkaram (7) taṭi (8) katti
vāraṇaṉ āyutam
vāraṇaṉ allatu varuṇaṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum teyvam piṟa ellāt teyvaṅkaḷiyum viṭa mikak kuṟivāṉa āyutaṅkaḷip peṟṟirukkiṟāṉ.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟākum:
1. pācam, 2. eṟi īṭṭi, 3. katti pōṉṟavikaḷ.
itteyvaṅkaḷil kāḷi (māyōḷ) mutalil kuṟiñci nilat teyvamāka iru kikaḷiyuṭiyavaḷāka kāḷiyāka iruntāḷ.  appoḻutu avaḷiṭam kompu, vil, ampu, kalvāḷ, kēṭayam ākiya āyutaṅkaḷē iruntaṉa.  appāl kuṟiñci makkaḷ uṇavu tēṭi mulli nilattil kuṭiyēṟiya poḻutu, annilattiṟkum teyvamāka irunta māyōḷ 6 kikaḷiyuṭiyavaḷāka vil ampu katti mutaliyavikaḷōṭu kati pācam pōṉṟa āyutaṅkaḷip peṟṟāḷ.  appāl makkaḷ uṇavu tēṭi maruta nilattilum, neytal nilattilum kuṭiyēṟiṉar.  maruta nilattil toḻil perukiyatu.  eṉavē aṅku patiṉāṟu kikaḷiyuṭiya pattira kāḷiyāy mākāḷiyāyk kalappi, paracu, cūlam, tiricūlam, īṭṭi, kivāḷ, vacciram, kēṭayam, aṅkucam,  kaṭvāṅkam mutaliya eṇṇaṟṟa āyutaṅkaḷit tāṅki marutam neytal maṭṭumalla ellā nilattiṟkum teyvamāṉāḷ.  appāl avaḷi maṇanta civa perumāṉ toḻil niṟinta maruta nilattiṟkut teyvamāki avaḷatu āyutaṅkaḷip peṟṟatōṭu toḻilkaḷukkuriya pala putiya āyutaṅkaḷip peṟṟu ulōkakālat teyvamāka eḻuntār.
pōrmuṟi
pōril vīrarkaḷ vil, vēl, īṭṭi, katti, eṟuḻ, taṇṭu, kokkari, cili, taṭi, tavar, kuṇil, muṉi pōṉṟa nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa āyutaṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.  avikaḷil kiviṭum paṭi kiviṭāppaṭi eṉa iruvaki uṇṭu.  vil nāṇil ampu vittu eyyum iṭam uṭu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu.  taṟkāppiṟku uriya āyutam kēṭayam ākum.  atu kiṭuku, kaṭakam, taṭṭu, parici, palaki, paṟi, vaṭṭaṇi, paṭṭam, tōl, tōl pāram eṉap pala vakip
paṭum.  pakivarkaḷiṉ īṭṭi allatu kaṇi, katti mutaliyaṉa uṭampittākkā vaṇṇam aṇiyum kavacam mey kākkum caṭṭi, mēlakam, araṇi, ācu eṉavum, kikkavacam kippuṭi eṉavum aḻikkappaṭṭatu.
nakariṉ atikārikaḷ paṭiyi naṭattuvar.  appoḻutu nam nāṭṭup paṭikaḷ aintu uṟuppiṉar aṭaṅkiya aintu vitap paṭikaḷ ākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. oṉṟu kaṭaṟpaṭiyōṭu ottuḻittatu.  maṟṟatu pōrukkuriya iyantiraṅkaḷiyum pōr vīrariṉ uṇaviyum piṟa poruḷkaḷiyum koṇṭu cellum vaṇṭikaḷiyum erutukaḷiyum kavaṉippatu.  itu vēliyāṭ kaḷiyum paṟi koṭṭuvōr, callari pōṭuvōr kutirip pākar, iyantiram paḻutu pārppōr avarkaḷiṉ utaviyāḷar ākiyavar kaḷi aḷikkiṟatu.  callari ōciyi aṟintu kutirikkup pul koṇṭu celvar.  mūṉṟāvatu pirivu tāṉip paṭikkum, nāṉ kāvatu pirivu kutirippaṭikkum aintāvatu pirivu tērp paṭikkum  poṟuppāka iruntatu.  aracarkaḷ āyutac cāliyi amit
tiruntaṉar.  āyutaṅkaḷi āyutac cāliyilum kutiriyi allatu yāṉiyip pantiyilum viṭṭuc celvar.  yāṉikkuk kaṭivāḷam iṭuvatilli.  tērkaḷik kāḷikaḷ iḻuttuc cellum.  tēri iḻuttuc celvatil kaḷippu eḻāta vaṇṇam kutirikaḷ pakkattē naṭattic cellappaṭṭaṉa.  tērppākaṉiṉ irupuṟamum iru vīrarkaḷ iruppar.  pōr yāṉi nāṉku vīrarkaḷi- atāvatu mūṉṟu pōr vīraṉiyum orupākaṉiyum koṇṭu cellum.
paṇṭikkālat tamiḻarkaḷiṉ pōr naṭavaṭikkiyil pakivar kaḷi etirttut tākkum pōrkkaruvikaḷip pōlavē taṟpātu kāppu āyutaṅkaḷum mika mukkiyamāṉavikaḷāyk karutappaṭṭaṉa.  pōril īṭupaṭṭa paṭivīraṉ oruvaṉuṭiya uṭampiṉ pātukāppu aṭippaṭiyāṉa uṭkaruttākum.  eṉavē avaṉ uṭalukku ūṟu viḷiyum ellāk koṭumiyiṉiṉṟu tappuvittuk koḷḷa ellāvitamāṉa muṉṉeccarikkiyāṉa naṭavaṭikkikaḷiyum eṭuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  eṉavē taṟpātukāppup paṭik kalaṅkaḷāka īṭṭikaḷ tuḷikka muṭiyāta mēlaṅki, talik kavacam kāppuk karuvikaḷ aṇintiruntaṉar.  itu tavira kavacam allatu pōrkkavacam mutaliyavikaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar.  itikācap pōr vīrarkaḷ tōl kēṭayaṅkaḷiyum, viraṟkāppuk karuvikaḷi yum pūṇṭiruntatāka aṟikiṟōm.  ivikaḷukku namatu ciṟpa nūṟ kaḷiliruntu nalla cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭa muṭiyum.
āriyarkaḷip paṟṟik kāytal uvattal iṉṟi ārāyntaṟinta aṟiñarkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷ āriyarkaḷiṭam tōlviyuṟṟa varalāṟṟuc ceytiyi aṟintu viyappuṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.  āṉāl cila vaṭa nāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ paṇpāṭum kalinayamum aṟivāṟṟalum mikka tirā viṭarkaḷ āriyarkaḷiṭam tōlviyuṟṟataṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷi naṉku ārāyntu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.
āriyarkaḷākiya nammip pārkkilum avarkaḷ (acurarkaḷ) keṭṭikkārarkaḷ.  avarkaḷuṭiya nakaraṅkaḷip pōl nakaraṅkaḷ amippataṟku nām kaṟṟuk koḷḷa nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷ piṭikkum. avarka ḷuṭiya kaṭivīti, tāmari malarkaḷ niṟintirukkum taṭākaṅkaḷ, ataṉ uyarnta paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ akaṉṟa irāca vītikaḷ pōṉṟa amippukaḷi nam āriya pūmiyil kāṇavē muṭiyātu.  nām acurarka ḷuṭiya purātaṉa nakaraṅkaḷiyum pārttirukkiṟōm. acurarkaḷ viṭṭuc ceṉṟa aḻintu pōṉa paḻiya nakaraṅkaḷi āriyarkaḷ paḻutu pārttut tirumpa amittirukkiṟārkaḷ.  āyiṉum avaṟṟilē paḻiya amippik kāṇa muṭiyavilli.  āṉāl intap putiya nakaratti aracaṉ campāṉ nērē iruntu kaṭṭiṉāṉām.  itu tēvalōkam pōl irukkiṟatu - (vālkāviliruntu kaṅkivari. pak 122)
elliyaṟṟa camuttirattilē acurarkaḷ taṅkaḷ periya paṭakukaḷi nirppayamākac celuttukiṟārkaḷ.  mātak kaṇakkilum varuṭak kaṇakkilum pirayāṇam ceytu kaṭaliliruntu vita vitamāṉa irattiṉaṅkaḷik koṇṭu varukiṟārkaḷ.  avarkaḷuṭiya tiriyattiṟkum tiṟamikkum itu oru eṭuttukkāṭṭu.  iṉṉoru aṟputam nī kēḷvippaṭṭirukkamāṭṭāy.  acurarkaḷ vāytiṟantu pēcāmalē campācikkiṟārkaḷ.  (eḻuttukkali aṟivi āciriyar iṅku kuṟippiṭukiṟār).  (vālkāviliruntu kaṅki vari. pak 123.)
nām iraṇṭumaṇinēram pēciyum viḷaṅka vikka muṭiyāta oru vicayatti (avaṉ (acuraṉ) iraṇṭu mūṉṟu kōṭukaḷi iḻuttu viḷaṅkavittu varukiṟāṉ. inta  vicayatti āriyar
kaḷākiya nām itu vari aṟiyamāṭṭōm. ippoḻutu tāṉ  nammuṭiya āriyarkaḷ intak kōṭukaḷik kaṟṟuk koḷḷa ārampittirukkiṟārkaḷ avarkaḷ iṉṉum pūraṇamākak kaṟṟuk
koḷḷavilli.
avarkaḷil kollar, taccar, taṭṭār, karumār, tantavēli ceyvōr, neyvōr, ciṟpikaḷ mutaliya ovvoru toḻi lāḷiyiṉuṭiya tiṟamiyiyum pārttu nām appaṭiyē ayarntu viṭukiṟōm.  appaṭippaṭṭa tiṟamicālikaḷi nammip pārk kilum keṭṭikkārarkaḷ eṉṟu oppuk koḷvatil āṭcēpaṇi eṉṉa?  - (vālkāviliruntu kaṅki vari. pak 124)
acurarkaḷ nilaṅkaḷiṉ ceḻumikku nalla ēṟpāṭu ceytiruk
kiṟārkaḷ.  avarkaḷ mali āṟukaḷiliruntu kālvāykaḷ veṭṭi nilaṅkaḷil nīrpāyccuvataṟku ēṟpāṭu ceytirukkiṟārkaḷ.  nām kāntārattil ivvaḻiyik kaṭippiṭittōm.  avarka ḷuṭiya nakar nirmāṇam maruttuvam mutaliyavikaḷ uyarnta muṟiyil iruntaṉa.
cuvāta natik kariyil āriyarkaḷum avarkaḷuṭiya muṉ ṉōrkaḷum pañcāṭiyiṉ peyarik kūṭak kēṭṭirukka māṭ ṭārkaḷ.  āṉāl nām (āriyarkaḷ) ippoḻutu anta āṭi kaḷi aṇintu koṇṭirukkiṟōm.  veyyiṟkālattil atu mikavum upayōkappaṭukiṟatu - (vālkāviliruntu kaṅki vari. pak 134.)
acurarkaḷuṭiya anta nakaram mikka aḻakuvāyntatu.  cuṭṭa ceṅkaliṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum araṇkaḷ, vīṭukaḷ, vīti amippu, cākkaṭi vacati, ciṟiya periya kuḷaṅkaḷ, taṭākaṅkaḷ mutaliya ellā vacatikaḷum annakarilē aḻakākac ceyyappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa.
puṭpakalāvati nakariliruntu (acurarkaḷ nakariliruntu) palavita māṉa  āparaṇaṅkaḷ, pañcāṭikaḷ, pōrkkaruvikaḷ mutaliya ettaṉiyō poruḷkaḷ cuvātanatip piratēcattil maṭṭumeṉṉa; ataṟku mēlum malippiratēcaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kuṭicikaḷukkuṅ kūṭac ceṉṟu paraviṉa. cuvātanatip piratēcattiṉ poṉṉiṟak kūntali yuṭiya āriya aḻakikaḷ acura ciṟpikaḷ ceyyum aṇikalaṉkaḷilē mayaṅkik kiṭantaṉar.  inta mayakkam āṇṭutōṟum atikamāṉa eṇṇikkiyil āriya aḻakikaḷi puṭkalāvati nakarattiṟku iḻuttuc ceṉṟatu - (vālkāviliruntu kaṅki vari. pak 113)
acurarkaḷiṭam cempu mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷ atikamuṇṭu.  āṉāl ulōkak kōṭṭi kaṭṭuvataṟkuriya cempukaḷ avarka ḷiṭam irukka muṭiyātu........  ceṅkaṟkaḷik koṇṭē avarkaḷ nakarattic cuṟṟiyum kaṉatta cuvar eḻuppik kōṭṭi kaṭṭiyiruk kiṟārkaḷ.  antac ceṅkaṟkaḷum civappu niṟattavitāṉ.
